{"parentUuid":"e3006dff-149a-4b12-b886-1ce5abd68150","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"268bd096-eea3-427d-901e-7ff4f0decc0a","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014L3WVWMajS5cfTA6EMwqa5","type":"tool_result","content":"# CONTEXT.md glossary\n\nProject vocabulary for the Kenyon College Finance Club (KCFC) website. One word per concept; if a better word appears, change it here.\n\n## People and roles\n\n**President**: One of the three students who run KCFC. Every President holds the Admin role.\n\n**Officer**: A club member other than a President who helps run the club. Every Officer holds the Editor role while in office.\n\n**Admin**: The role held by the three Presidents. Can create, edit, publish and delete Posts and Pages, and manage Users.\n\n**Editor**: The role held by Officers. Can create and edit Posts and save them as Drafts. Cannot publish, cannot delete a published Post, cannot touch Pages, and cannot see User management. An Editor sees and edits only their own Posts, so a sector team's writeup changes only through its own author until a President reviews it. Admins see and edit every Post.\n\n**User**: Anyone with an individual account on the admin console. Only Presidents and Officers are Users. Never shared between people.\n\n**Visitor**: Anyone reading the public site: prospective members, current members, alumni, recruiters. Visitors never log in, and everything on the public site is visible to all of them. There is no members-only content.\n\n**Sign-in**: A User signs in with their Kenyon email address and a password. A President creates the account and sets the first password face to face; there is no forced change at first sign-in. A lost password is reset by an Admin in person, never by email.\n\n**Idle sign-out**: A User is signed out automatically after about 2 hours without activity, because the Club laptop is shared at meetings.\n\n**Deactivated**: A User switched off by an Admin when their time in office ends. They can no longer sign in, and any open session ends at their next action. Their Posts keep their name as author. An Admin can reactivate them. Users are never deleted. At least one President must always stay active, and a President cannot deactivate themselves.\n\n## Content\n\n**Post**: The one kind of dated club material: a meeting announcement, a pitch writeup, or an alumni call recap. There is a single kind of Post and no categories.\n\n**Pitch writeup**: A Post presenting a stock pitch. Spring pitches are group work by sector teams, so the team members are listed in the body text. It stays a Draft until a President has reviewed it.\n\n**Sector team**: One of the three groups in the spring stock pitch competition, each covering a sector and led by one President. Teams present to Kenyon alumni working in equity research at William Blair.\n\n**Analyst Program**: The club's program for newer members. Its lasting description is an Admin-only Page.\n\n**Page**: Lasting club information: About, Leadership, Analyst Program, Join, Recruiting. Only Admins may create, edit or delete a Page; Editors cannot touch them at all.\n\n**Join Page**: The Page for prospective members: what the club does, whether prior experience is needed, how to come to a meeting.\n\n**Recruiting Page**: The Page for current members: the resume review form link and standing guides. Kept separate from the Join Page (one Page, one audience).\n\n**Homepage**: A Page edited by Admins. The next meeting (every other week, Oden 110) is the first thing a Visitor sees, followed by the contact email and the latest Posts.\n\n**Next-meeting line**: Plain text at the top of the Homepage, e.g. \"Next meeting: Wed Oct 28, 7 pm, Oden 110\". A President updates it the night before each meeting, the same habit as the email reminder. There is no calendar or automatic date logic.\n\n**Navigation**: The public menu. It lists Published Pages in an order an Admin sets, plus a link to the Posts listing.\n\n**Byline**: The public line on a Post, \"By <name>, <date>\", naming whoever posted it, never an email address. A Deactivated User's name still shows on their old Posts. Pages show no byline. Group work (such as the spring pitches by three sector teams, each led by a President) has no team field: the poster is the byline and the team members are listed in the body text.\n\n**Posts listing**: The public list of Published Posts, newest first, showing title and date.\n\n**Title convention**: A club habit, not enforced by the system: Post titles for pitches and recaps start with \"Pitch:\" or \"Recap:\" (e.g. \"Pitch: NVDA, Spring 2026\") so the Posts listing is scannable. Admins hand-link standout archive Posts from the Recruiting Page.\n\n**Event**: Not a separate thing. An event is announced in a Post.\n\n**Slug**: The short address-name of a Post or Page (e.g. `spring-stock-pitch`). Made from the title once, when first saved, and unchanged when the title is edited. Only an Admin can edit it; an old slug is not redirected.\n\n**Unpublish**: An Admin returns a Published Post or Page to Draft. It leaves the public site at the next Publish run but is kept.\n\n**Delete**: Permanent removal of a Post or Page, after an \"are you sure?\" step. Only an Admin can delete a Published Post or any Page; an Editor can delete only their own Drafts. Users are never deleted, only Deactivated (for example when seniors graduate in May: their old Posts keep their name, but they cannot log in).\n\n**Draft**: A Post that has been saved but not published. Visible only to signed-in Users, never on the public site. A Published Post returns to Draft when an Editor edits it, and stays out of the public site until an Admin publishes it again. An Admin's edit leaves it Published.\n\n**Published**: The status of a Post or Page that an Admin has approved for the public site. Approved is not the same as Live.\n\n**Publish run**: Running `cms publish`, which only an Admin does, usually right before a meeting. It rebuilds the public site from everything Published and deploys it, with no change summary. Marking an item Published has a simple \"Publish this?\" confirm on the button. A mistake is fixed by unpublishing it and running it again.\n\n**Live**: On the public site as of the last Publish run. A Published item becomes Live only at the next Publish run.\n\n**Pending changes**: Changes made since the last Publish run, which the console shows in a banner so nobody assumes saved work is already Live.\n\n**Dashboard**: The console's first screen. Shows counts of Drafts, Published items and Pending changes (Admins also see active and Deactivated Users), and a one-click \"New Post\" button.\n\n**Content list**: The console's list of Posts and Pages, newest first, filterable by status (with counts) and type. Editors see Posts only.\n\n**Preview**: The editor's side-by-side view of how the sanitized Markdown will look on the public site, labelled \"Draft, not Live\" when it is not.\n\n**Club laptop**: The one computer that runs the admin console. The console is never on the public internet; Officers sign in on it with their own accounts.\n\n## Out of scope\n\nComments, image uploads, categories, scheduled posts, email newsletters, member logins.\ntitle:\tSpec: KCFC web CMS\nstate:\tOPEN\nauthor:\tiaishikb-rgb\nlabels:\tready-for-agent, spec\ncomments:\t0\nassignees:\t\nprojects:\t\nmilestone:\t\nissue-type:\t\nparent:\t\nsub-issues:\t\nsub-issues-completed:\t\nblocked-by:\t\nblocking:\t\nnumber:\t1\n--\n## Problem Statement\n\nThe Kenyon College Finance Club (KCFC) is run by three Presidents and about eight Officers, and nearly everything is communicated by email to financeclub@kenyon.edu. The current Wix site shows no officers, no meeting time and no events, its Contact Us link goes back to the homepage, and its Resources headings have nothing under them, so a first-year or an alumnus can reasonably conclude the club is dead. Only one or two people can update it, there is no way to give Officers limited access, and nothing stops an unfinished stock pitch from going out under the club's name in front of William Blair alumni. When leadership changes there is no clean way to hand over or take away access.\n\nVocabulary below is defined in `CONTEXT.md`.\n\n## Users\n\n- **Presidents** (3, Admin role): run the club, review everything, sign off on what goes public.\n- **Officers** (about 8, Editor role): contribute meeting announcements, pitch writeups and alumni call recaps. Not technical; an announcement must take under two minutes.\n- **Visitors** (never log in): prospective members (first-years and sophomores) asking \"what is this, do I need experience, when and where do you meet\"; current members looking for event details, the resume review form and the Analyst Program; alumni and recruiters (including William Blair) checking leadership and activity before an event or pitch review.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS with a local admin console on the Club laptop and a static public site. Presidents and Officers sign in with individual accounts. Officers write Posts and save Drafts; only Presidents publish, manage Pages, and manage Users. A Publish run turns everything Published into a static site on GitHub Pages. The first thing a Visitor sees is the next meeting. Nothing unreviewed reaches the public site, and an Officer who leaves loses access without anyone changing a shared password.\n\n## The 7 required capabilities, in KCFC's words\n\n1. **Login.** Presidents and Officers sign in and out with their Kenyon email and their own password. Passwords are stored hashed, never as text. A President sets the first password face to face, and resets lost passwords in person. Signed in on the shared Club laptop, a User is signed out after about 2 hours idle.\n2. **Two roles.** *Admin* (the three Presidents) can do everything. *Editor* (Officers) can write and edit only their own Posts, save them as Drafts and delete their own Drafts. An Editor cannot publish, cannot delete a Published Post, cannot touch any Page, and cannot see user management. These limits are enforced by the system itself, including when an Editor types a restricted address straight into the browser (the WordPress \"Editor can trash the About page\" problem from the field notes).\n3. **Posts.** One kind of Post (meeting announcement, pitch writeup, or alumni call recap), no categories. Each has a title, slug, Markdown body, Draft/Published status, author and timestamps, and can be created, read, updated and deleted under the role rules above. The slug is made from the title once and never changes when the title is edited; only an Admin can edit it. An Editor's edit to a Published Post turns it back into a Draft until a President republishes it. An Admin's edit leaves it Published.\n4. **Pages.** Lasting club information: Homepage, About, Leadership, Analyst Program, Join (for prospective members), Recruiting (for current members: resume review form link, standing guides). Same fields as a Post, but only Admins may create, edit, or delete them. Published Pages appear in the public Navigation in an order an Admin sets. The Homepage opens with the **next-meeting line** (plain text such as \"Next meeting: Wed Oct 28, 7 pm, Oden 110\", updated by a President the night before), then the contact email, then the latest Posts.\n5. **Admin console.** A dashboard with counts of Drafts, Published items and Pending changes (Admins also see active and Deactivated User counts), a one-click \"New Post\" button, and a \"pending changes\" banner so nobody assumes saved work is already Live. A content list, newest first, filterable by status (All / Published / Draft, with counts) and type; Editors see only their own Posts. An editor with a side-by-side Preview of the sanitized Markdown, labelled \"Draft, not Live\" when it is not.\n6. **User management (Admin only).** Presidents create Users, change Roles, and Deactivate Users. A Deactivated User cannot sign in, and an open session ends at their next action. Users are never deleted: when seniors graduate in May their old Posts keep their name but they cannot log in. A Deactivated User can be reactivated. At least one President must always stay active, and a President cannot deactivate themselves.\n7. **Public site and publish.** Visitors see only Published content. Markdown is sanitized (see below). Navigation is built from Published Pages plus a link to the Posts listing (newest first, title and date). Each Post shows a byline, \"By <name>, <date>\", never an email address; Pages show none. `cms publish` (Admin only, usually run right before a meeting) writes `site/` with relative paths only and deploys it to GitHub Pages. Marking an item Published has a \"Publish this?\" confirm. There is no change summary; a mistake is fixed by unpublishing and running it again.\n\n## User Stories\n\n1. As a first-year, I want the next meeting's date, time and place at the very top of the homepage, so that I know when and where to show up.\n2. As a prospective member, I want a Join page saying what the club does and whether I need experience, so that I can decide whether to come.\n3. As a prospective member, I want the contact email on the homepage, so that I can ask a question.\n4. As a current member, I want a Recruiting page with the resume review form link, so that I can request a review without digging through email.\n5. As a current member, I want a list of Posts, newest first, so that I can find the latest announcement.\n6. As a current member, I want to read about the Analyst Program on its own page, so that I know what it involves.\n7. As an alumnus, I want a Leadership page, so that I know who runs the club before I speak at an event.\n8. As a recruiter or William Blair alumnus, I want to read reviewed pitch writeups and recaps, so that I can see the club's work, and I want to see only reviewed work.\n9. As a Visitor, I want links that keep working, so that a shared link to a Post is never dead.\n10. As a Visitor, I want to see an author's name but never their email address, so that student contact details stay private.\n11. As a Visitor, I want the site to work when served from a GitHub Pages subfolder, so that no page or link is broken.\n12. As an Officer, I want my own account, so that I never share a password.\n13. As an Officer, I want to sign in with my Kenyon email, so that it is easy to remember.\n14. As an Officer, I want to write a meeting announcement in under two minutes, so that updating the site is not a chore.\n15. As an Officer, I want to write in plain Markdown with a preview beside it, so that I can see what the Post will look like.\n16. As an Officer, I want to save my work as a Draft, so that I can finish it later.\n17. As an Officer, I want my Draft to stay private until a President publishes it, so that nothing goes public by accident.\n18. As an Officer, I want to see and edit only my own Posts, so that I do not break another team's pitch before the William Blair presentation.\n19. As an Officer, I want to delete my own abandoned Drafts, so that my list stays clean.\n20. As an Officer, I want to be told clearly when I cannot do something, so that I do not think the site is broken.\n21. As an Officer, I want a \"Draft, not Live\" label on my preview, so that I do not think members can already see it.\n22. As an Officer, I want to be signed out automatically on the shared laptop, so that the next person cannot use my account.\n23. As an Officer, I want my name on my Post after I graduate, so that my work keeps its credit.\n24. As a President, I want to review every Draft in one list, so that I can sign off before publishing.\n25. As a President, I want to see all Drafts filtered by status with counts, so that I know what still needs attention.\n26. As a President, I want to edit any Post, so that I can fix a pitch writeup before it goes out.\n27. As a President, I want an Officer's edit to a Published Post to turn it back to Draft, so that no unreviewed change goes out.\n28. As a President, I want a \"Publish this?\" confirm, so that I do not publish by mis-click.\n29. As a President, I want to unpublish a Post or Page, so that I can take down a mistake and rerun the publish.\n30. As a President, I want to delete a Post or Page after an \"are you sure?\" step, so that deletion is deliberate.\n31. As a President, I want only Presidents to edit or delete Leadership and Analyst Program (and every other Page), so that Officers cannot damage official pages.\n32. As a President, I want to set the order of Pages in Navigation, so that the menu reads sensibly.\n33. As a President, I want to edit the Homepage's next-meeting line, so that the first thing a first-year sees is current.\n34. As a President, I want a banner showing pending changes, so that I know the live site is behind.\n35. As a President, I want to run `cms publish` right before a meeting, so that the public site is up to date.\n36. As an Officer, I want to delete my own abandoned Drafts, so that my list stays clean.\n20. As an Officer, I want to be told clearly when I cannot do something, so that I do not think the site is broken.\n21. As an Officer, I want a \"Draft, not Live\" label on my preview, so that I do not think members can already see it.\n22. As an Officer, I want to be signed out automatically on the shared laptop, so that the next person cannot use my account.\n23. As an Officer, I want my name on my Post after I graduate, so that my work keeps its credit.\n24. As a President, I want to review every Draft in one list, so that I can sign off before publishing.\n25. As a President, I want to see all Drafts filtered by status with counts, so that I know what still needs attention.\n26. As a President, I want to edit any Post, so that I can fix a pitch writeup before it goes out.\n27. As a President, I want an Officer's edit to a Published Post to turn it back to Draft, so that no unreviewed change goes out.\n28. As a President, I want a \"Publish this?\" confirm, so that I do not publish by mis-click.\n29. As a President, I want to unpublish a Post or Page, so that I can take down a mistake and rerun the publish.\n30. As a President, I want to delete a Post or Page after an \"are you sure?\" step, so that deletion is deliberate.\n31. As a President, I want only Presidents to edit or delete Leadership and Analyst Program (and every other Page), so that Officers cannot damage official pages.\n32. As a President, I want to set the order of Pages in Navigation, so that the menu reads sensibly.\n33. As a President, I want to edit the Homepage's next-meeting line, so that the first thing a first-year sees is current.\n34. As a President, I want a banner showing pending changes, so that I know the live site is behind.\n35. As a President, I want to run `cms publish` right before a meeting, so that the public site is up to date.\n36. As a President, I want to create accounts and set the first password in person, so that no email system is required.\napp/auth.py\napp/db.py\napp/templating.py\napp/users.py\napp/posts.py\napp/__init__.py\napp/seed.py\napp/pages.py\napp/cli.py\napp/markdown.py\napp/settings.py\napp/main.py\napp/publish.py\ntemplates/base.html\ntests/test_t01_login.py\ntests/test_t02_posts.py\ntests/test_t04_dashboard.py\ntests/conftest.py\ntests/test_t03_president_control.py\ntests/test_usage_report.py\ntests/test_ctx_guard.py\ntests/test_t05_pages.py\ntests/test_t00.py\ntests/test_secret_key.py\napp/routes/auth.py\napp/routes/posts.py\napp/routes/__init__.py\napp/routes/pages.py\napp/routes/admin.py\ntemplates/admin/page_confirm.html\ntemplates/admin/post_confirm.html\ntemplates/admin/page_delete.html\ntemplates/admin/login.html\ntemplates/admin/hello.html\ntemplates/admin/post_list.html\ntemplates/admin/post_delete.html\ntemplates/admin/page_edit.html\ntemplates/admin/post_edit.html\ntemplates/public/home.html\n       6 app/__init__.py\n      84 app/auth.py\n      60 app/cli.py\n      62 app/db.py\n      66 app/main.py\n      21 app/markdown.py\n     108 app/pages.py\n     115 app/posts.py\n      47 app/publish.py\n      23 app/seed.py\n      36 app/settings.py\n       9 app/templating.py\n      78 app/users.py\n     715 total","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e3006dff-149a-4b12-b886-1ce5abd68150","permissionDecision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reasonType":"mode"},"session_id":"93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14","version":"2.1.300","gitBranch":"main"}

{"type":"api-request-blob","sessionId":"93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14","hash":"29ec87c756f588534a0db55926d983788d80e8f1059a0a79be2defd8172ca7dc","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017szMBiaGjDpR7qDJD4AUF3","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Sessions, CSRF and the permission guard. Every admin route goes through here.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\nfrom jinja2 import pass_context\n\nfrom app import db, settings, users\n\nSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\nclass NotSignedIn(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised by the guard; the app turns it into a redirect to /login.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef ensure_csrf(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n        request.session[\"csrf\"] = token\n    return token\n\n\n@pass_context\ndef csrf_token(context) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Use as {{ csrf_token() }} inside every state-changing form.\"\"\"\n    return ensure_csrf(context[\"request\"])\n\n\nasync def csrf_protect(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"App-wide: any request that is not GET/HEAD/OPTIONS needs a valid token.\"\"\"\n    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    if request.url.path.startswith(\"/admin\") and \"user_id\" not in request.session:\n        raise NotSignedIn  # anonymous: send to login rather than a CSRF error\n    form = await request.form()\n    sent = str(form.get(\"csrf_token\", \"\"))\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\", \"\")\n    if not expected or not secrets.compare_digest(sent, expected):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Invalid or missing CSRF token\")\n\n\ndef now(request: Request) -> float:\n    return request.app.state.clock()\n\n\ndef start_session(request: Request, user: users.User) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()  # fresh session on sign-in\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user.id\n    request.session[\"last_seen\"] = now(request)\n    ensure_csrf(request)\n\n\ndef end_session(request: Request) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()\n\n\ndef require_user(request: Request, conn=Depends(db.get_db)) -> users.User:\n    \"\"\"Signed in, not idle too long, and still active; otherwise to the login page.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    last_seen = request.session.get(\"last_seen\")\n    current = now(request)\n    if user_id is None or last_seen is None or current - last_seen > settings.IDLE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:\n        end_session(request)\n        raise NotSignedIn\n    user = users.get_user(conn, user_id)\n    if user is None or not user.active:\n        end_session(request)\n        raise NotSignedIn\n    request.session[\"last_seen\"] = current\n    return user\n\n\ndef require_role(*roles: str):\n    \"\"\"Guard for role-restricted routes: Depends(require_role(\"admin\")).\"\"\"\n\n    def guard(user: users.User = Depends(require_user)) -> users.User:\n        if user.role not in roles:\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"You do not have access to this page\")\n        return user\n\n    return guard\n\"\"\"Users: argon2-hashed passwords, roles, the active flag.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom functools import cache\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass User:\n    id: int\n    email: str\n    role: str  # \"admin\" (President) or \"editor\" (Officer)\n    active: bool\n\n\ndef normalize_email(email: str) -> str:\n    return email.strip().lower()\n\n\ndef _row_to_user(row: sqlite3.Row) -> User:\n    return User(row[\"id\"], row[\"email\"], row[\"role\"], bool(row[\"active\"]))\n\n\ndef create_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str, password: str, role: str) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO users (email, password_hash, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\",\n            (normalize_email(email), _hasher.hash(password), role),\n        )\n\n\ndef get_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> User | None:\n    row = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()\n    return _row_to_user(row) if row else None\n\n\ndef count_by_active(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> tuple[int, int]:\n    \"\"\"(active, Deactivated) User counts.\"\"\"\n    row = conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT COALESCE(SUM(active), 0) AS a, COUNT(*) AS n FROM users\").fetchone()\n    return row[\"a\"], row[\"n\"] - row[\"a\"]\n\n\ndef email_exists(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (normalize_email(email),)\n    ).fetchone() is not None\n\n\n@cache\ndef _dummy_hash() -> str:\n    return _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")\n\n\ndef authenticate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str, password: str) -> User | None:\n    \"\"\"Return the User for a correct email and password, else None.\n\n    Unknown email, wrong password and Deactivated all look the same to the\n    caller, and unknown emails still cost one hash check so timing does not\n    reveal which accounts exist.\n    \"\"\"\n    row = conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (normalize_email(email),)\n    ).fetchone()\n    stored = row[\"password_hash\"] if row else _dummy_hash()\n    try:\n        _hasher.verify(stored, password)\n    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):\n        return None\n    if row is None or not row[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return _row_to_user(row)\n\"\"\"SQLite access. One short-lived connection per request.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Request\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    email         TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n    role          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    title      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    slug       TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    body       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id  INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n    created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n    updated_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pages (\n    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    title      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    slug       TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    body       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    nav_order  INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n    updated_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(path, check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    with conn:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n    conn.close()\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    \"\"\"FastAPI dependency: a connection for this request, closed afterwards.\"\"\"\n    conn = connect(request.app.state.database)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nThe admin console answers at /admin and the public site answers at /. Sign-in,\nsessions, CSRF and the permission guard live in app/auth.py; add routes in their\nown modules (app/routes/...) and include them here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport time\nfrom contextlib import asynccontextmanager\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Callable\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import auth, db, settings\nfrom app.routes import admin as admin_routes\nfrom app.routes import auth as auth_routes\nfrom app.routes import pages as page_routes\nfrom app.routes import posts as post_routes\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\n\ndef create_app(database: Path | None = None,\n               clock: Callable[[], float] | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    settings.require_safe_secret_key()\n    database = Path(database or settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n\n    @asynccontextmanager\n    async def lifespan(_: FastAPI):\n        db.init_db(database)\n        yield\n\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\", lifespan=lifespan,\n                  dependencies=[Depends(auth.csrf_protect)])\n    app.state.database = database\n    app.state.clock = clock or time.time\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       session_cookie=\"cms_session\", same_site=\"lax\",\n                       max_age=None)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(auth.NotSignedIn)\n    async def to_login(request: Request, exc: auth.NotSignedIn):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\n    app.include_router(auth_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(admin_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(post_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(page_routes.router)\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Later ticket routers plug in here, above the catch-all.\n    app.include_router(admin_routes.catch_all)  # keep LAST\n\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n\"\"\"The admin console's home page and the /admin catch-all.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import auth, db, pages, posts, users\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\")\n\n# Include this router LAST: an unknown /admin address still sends anonymous\n# visitors to the login page instead of a bare 404.\ncatch_all = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef admin_home(request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    is_admin = user.role == \"admin\"\n    counts = posts.count_by_status(conn, None if is_admin else user.id)\n    if is_admin:\n        for status, n in pages.count_by_status(conn).items():\n            counts[status] += n\n    active, deactivated = users.count_by_active(conn) if is_admin else (None, None)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/hello.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Admin\", \"user\": user, \"drafts\": counts[posts.DRAFT],\n         \"published\": counts[posts.PUBLISHED], \"active_users\": active,\n         \"deactivated_users\": deactivated},\n    )\n\n\n@catch_all.api_route(\"/{rest:path}\", include_in_schema=False,\n                     methods=[\"GET\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"PATCH\", \"DELETE\"],\n                     dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_user)])\ndef admin_unknown(rest: str):\n    raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n\"\"\"Console routes for Pages. President-only: every route is behind the admin guard.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import auth, db, pages\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.posts import DRAFT, PUBLISHED, slugify\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/pages\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_role(\"admin\"))])\n\n\nNAV_ERROR = \"Navigation order must be a whole number, 0 or more\"\n\n\ndef _page(conn, page_id: int) -> pages.Page:\n    page = pages.get_page(conn, page_id)\n    if page is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No such Page\")\n    return page\n\n\ndef _int(value: str) -> int:\n    \"\"\"The navigation order as a number; anything that is not 0 or more counts as -1.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        return max(int(value.strip() or 0), -1)\n    except ValueError:\n        return -1\n\n\ndef _editor(request, user, page, title, body, nav_order, error=None, status_code=200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/page_edit.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit Page\" if page else \"New Page\", \"user\": user, \"page\": page,\n         \"form_title\": title, \"form_body\": body, \"form_nav_order\": nav_order,\n         \"preview_html\": render_markdown(body), \"error\": error},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef page_list():\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts?type=page\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef page_new(request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user)):\n    return _editor(request, user, None, \"\", \"\", 0)\n\n\n@router.post(\"\")\ndef page_create(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                nav_order: str = Form(\"0\"), user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        return _editor(request, user, None, title, body, nav_order, \"A Page needs a title\", 400)\n    if _int(nav_order) < 0:\n        return _editor(request, user, None, title, body, nav_order, NAV_ERROR, 400)\n    page_id = pages.create_page(conn, title, body, _int(nav_order), auth.now(request))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}\")\ndef page_edit(page_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n              conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    page = _page(conn, page_id)\n    return _editor(request, user, page, page.title, page.body, page.nav_order)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}\")\ndef page_update(page_id: int, request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                nav_order: str = Form(\"0\"), slug: str | None = Form(None),\n                user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    page = _page(conn, page_id)\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        return _editor(request, user, page, title, body, nav_order, \"A Page needs a title\", 400)\n    if _int(nav_order) < 0:\n        return _editor(request, user, page, title, body, nav_order, NAV_ERROR, 400)\n    new_slug = slugify(slug) if slug and slug.strip() else None\n    if new_slug and pages.slug_taken(conn, new_slug, page_id):\n        return _editor(request, user, page, title, body, _int(nav_order),\n                       f\"The slug “{new_slug}” is already used by another Page\", 400)\n    pages.update_page(conn, page_id, title, body, _int(nav_order), auth.now(request), new_slug)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\nCONFIRMS = {\n    \"publish\": (\"Publish this?\", \"Yes, publish it\", DRAFT, PUBLISHED,\n                \"It becomes Live at the next Publish run.\"),\n    \"unpublish\": (\"Are you sure you want to unpublish this?\", \"Yes, unpublish it\",\n                  PUBLISHED, DRAFT,\n                  \"It returns to Draft and leaves the public site at the next Publish run.\"),\n}\n\n\ndef _confirm(action: str, page_id: int, request, user, conn):\n    heading, button, _, _, note = CONFIRMS[action]\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/page_confirm.html\",\n        {\"title\": heading, \"user\": user, \"page\": _page(conn, page_id), \"action\": action,\n         \"heading\": heading, \"button\": button, \"note\": note})\n\n\ndef _change(action: str, page_id: int, request, conn):\n    _, _, from_status, to_status, _ = CONFIRMS[action]\n    if _page(conn, page_id).status == from_status:\n        pages.set_status(conn, page_id, to_status, auth.now(request))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}/publish\")\ndef page_publish_confirm(page_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                         conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _confirm(\"publish\", page_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}/publish\")\ndef page_publish(page_id: int, request: Request, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change(\"publish\", page_id, request, conn)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}/unpublish\")\ndef page_unpublish_confirm(page_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                           conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _confirm(\"unpublish\", page_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}/unpublish\")\ndef page_unpublish(page_id: int, request: Request, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change(\"unpublish\", page_id, request, conn)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}/delete\")\ndef page_delete_confirm(page_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                        conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/page_delete.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Delete Page\", \"user\": user, \"page\": _page(conn, page_id)})\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}/delete\")\ndef page_delete(page_id: int, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    pages.delete_page(conn, _page(conn, page_id).id)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts?type=page\", status_code=303)\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\nclass FakeClock:\n    \"\"\"Injected clock so idle sign-out is tested without waiting.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, now: float = 1_000_000.0):\n        self.now = now\n\n    def __call__(self) -> float:\n        return self.now\n\n    def advance(self, seconds: float) -> None:\n        self.now += seconds\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef clock() -> FakeClock:\n    return FakeClock()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef make_app(tmp_path, clock):\n    \"\"\"Factory for a fresh app on its own seeded database and the fake clock.\"\"\"\n    from app.seed import seed_users\n\n    def _make():\n        db = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n        application = create_app(database=db, clock=clock)\n        seed_users(db, {role: (u[\"email\"], u[\"password\"])\n                        for role, u in DEMO_USERS.items()})\n        return application\n\n    return _make\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(make_app) -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(make_app())\n\n\ndef csrf_of(client: TestClient, path: str = \"/login\") -> str:\n    \"\"\"Read the CSRF token out of the form on `path`.\"\"\"\n    html = client.get(path).text\n    return re.search(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"', html).group(1)\n\n\ndef sign_in(client: TestClient, email: str, password: str, token: str | None = None):\n    return client.post(\n        \"/login\",\n        data={\"email\": email, \"password\": password,\n              \"csrf_token\": token or csrf_of(client)},\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(make_app):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(make_app())\n        response = sign_in(c, user[\"email\"], user[\"password\"])\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %}</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"{{ css_path | default('style.css') }}\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header><a href=\"{{ home_path | default('index.html') }}\">{{ title }}</a></header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n  <footer><small>IPHS 400 Mini-Project #2</small></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Delete this Page?</h1>\n  <p>Are you sure you want to permanently delete “{{ page.title }}”? This cannot be undone.</p>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/pages/{{ page.id }}/delete\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Yes, delete it</button>\n    <a href=\"/admin/pages/{{ page.id }}\">Cancel</a>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\n\"\"\"T05: President-only Pages. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nfrom app import db, pages\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of\nfrom tests.test_t03_president_control import act, president_for\n\n\ndef create_page(c, title=\"About\", body=\"We are KCFC.\", order=\"2\"):\n    token = csrf_of(c, \"/admin/pages/new\")\n    return c.post(\"/admin/pages\", data={\"title\": title, \"body\": body, \"nav_order\": order,\n                                        \"csrf_token\": token}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef page_url(response) -> str:\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    return response.headers[\"location\"]\n\n\ndef test_president_creates_edits_and_sees_a_page(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    url = page_url(create_page(admin))\n    assert re.fullmatch(r\"/admin/pages/\\d+\", url)\n    html = admin.get(url).text\n    assert \"About\" in html and \"Status: Draft\" in html and \"about\" in html\n    token = csrf_of(admin, url)\n    admin.post(url, data={\"title\": \"About us\", \"body\": \"New\", \"nav_order\": \"5\",\n                          \"csrf_token\": token}, follow_redirects=False)\n    html = admin.get(url).text\n    assert \"About us\" in html and 'value=\"5\"' in html\n\n\ndef test_slug_is_generated_once_and_editable_by_president(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    url = page_url(create_page(admin, title=\"Join Us\"))\n    token = csrf_of(admin, url)\n    admin.post(url, data={\"title\": \"Join the club\", \"body\": \"\", \"nav_order\": \"1\",\n                          \"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert 'value=\"join-us\"' in admin.get(url).text\n    admin.post(url, data={\"title\": \"Join the club\", \"body\": \"\", \"nav_order\": \"1\",\n                          \"slug\": \"Get Involved\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert 'value=\"get-involved\"' in admin.get(url).text\n\n\ndef test_navigation_order_is_stored_on_the_page(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    url = page_url(create_page(admin, order=\"7\"))\n    conn = db.connect(admin.app.state.database)\n    pid = int(url.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[1])\n    assert pages.get_page(conn, pid).nav_order == 7\ndomain.md\nissue-tracker.md\ntriage-labels.md","is_error":false}]}}

{"parentUuid":"5a62e49e-8ab4-4254-aba3-e4ae1e5595ee","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"268bd096-eea3-427d-901e-7ff4f0decc0a","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017szMBiaGjDpR7qDJD4AUF3","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Sessions, CSRF and the permission guard. Every admin route goes through here.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\nfrom jinja2 import pass_context\n\nfrom app import db, settings, users\n\nSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\nclass NotSignedIn(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised by the guard; the app turns it into a redirect to /login.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef ensure_csrf(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n        request.session[\"csrf\"] = token\n    return token\n\n\n@pass_context\ndef csrf_token(context) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Use as {{ csrf_token() }} inside every state-changing form.\"\"\"\n    return ensure_csrf(context[\"request\"])\n\n\nasync def csrf_protect(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"App-wide: any request that is not GET/HEAD/OPTIONS needs a valid token.\"\"\"\n    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    if request.url.path.startswith(\"/admin\") and \"user_id\" not in request.session:\n        raise NotSignedIn  # anonymous: send to login rather than a CSRF error\n    form = await request.form()\n    sent = str(form.get(\"csrf_token\", \"\"))\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\", \"\")\n    if not expected or not secrets.compare_digest(sent, expected):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Invalid or missing CSRF token\")\n\n\ndef now(request: Request) -> float:\n    return request.app.state.clock()\n\n\ndef start_session(request: Request, user: users.User) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()  # fresh session on sign-in\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user.id\n    request.session[\"last_seen\"] = now(request)\n    ensure_csrf(request)\n\n\ndef end_session(request: Request) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()\n\n\ndef require_user(request: Request, conn=Depends(db.get_db)) -> users.User:\n    \"\"\"Signed in, not idle too long, and still active; otherwise to the login page.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    last_seen = request.session.get(\"last_seen\")\n    current = now(request)\n    if user_id is None or last_seen is None or current - last_seen > settings.IDLE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:\n        end_session(request)\n        raise NotSignedIn\n    user = users.get_user(conn, user_id)\n    if user is None or not user.active:\n        end_session(request)\n        raise NotSignedIn\n    request.session[\"last_seen\"] = current\n    return user\n\n\ndef require_role(*roles: str):\n    \"\"\"Guard for role-restricted routes: Depends(require_role(\"admin\")).\"\"\"\n\n    def guard(user: users.User = Depends(require_user)) -> users.User:\n        if user.role not in roles:\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"You do not have access to this page\")\n        return user\n\n    return guard\n\"\"\"Users: argon2-hashed passwords, roles, the active flag.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom functools import cache\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass User:\n    id: int\n    email: str\n    role: str  # \"admin\" (President) or \"editor\" (Officer)\n    active: bool\n\n\ndef normalize_email(email: str) -> str:\n    return email.strip().lower()\n\n\ndef _row_to_user(row: sqlite3.Row) -> User:\n    return User(row[\"id\"], row[\"email\"], row[\"role\"], bool(row[\"active\"]))\n\n\ndef create_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str, password: str, role: str) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO users (email, password_hash, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\",\n            (normalize_email(email), _hasher.hash(password), role),\n        )\n\n\ndef get_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> User | None:\n    row = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()\n    return _row_to_user(row) if row else None\n\n\ndef count_by_active(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> tuple[int, int]:\n    \"\"\"(active, Deactivated) User counts.\"\"\"\n    row = conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT COALESCE(SUM(active), 0) AS a, COUNT(*) AS n FROM users\").fetchone()\n    return row[\"a\"], row[\"n\"] - row[\"a\"]\n\n\ndef email_exists(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (normalize_email(email),)\n    ).fetchone() is not None\n\n\n@cache\ndef _dummy_hash() -> str:\n    return _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")\n\n\ndef authenticate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str, password: str) -> User | None:\n    \"\"\"Return the User for a correct email and password, else None.\n\n    Unknown email, wrong password and Deactivated all look the same to the\n    caller, and unknown emails still cost one hash check so timing does not\n    reveal which accounts exist.\n    \"\"\"\n    row = conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (normalize_email(email),)\n    ).fetchone()\n    stored = row[\"password_hash\"] if row else _dummy_hash()\n    try:\n        _hasher.verify(stored, password)\n    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):\n        return None\n    if row is None or not row[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return _row_to_user(row)\n\"\"\"SQLite access. One short-lived connection per request.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Request\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    email         TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n    role          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    title      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    slug       TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    body       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id  INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n    created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n    updated_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pages (\n    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    title      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    slug       TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    body       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    nav_order  INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n    updated_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(path, check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    with conn:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n    conn.close()\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    \"\"\"FastAPI dependency: a connection for this request, closed afterwards.\"\"\"\n    conn = connect(request.app.state.database)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nThe admin console answers at /admin and the public site answers at /. Sign-in,\nsessions, CSRF and the permission guard live in app/auth.py; add routes in their\nown modules (app/routes/...) and include them here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport time\nfrom contextlib import asynccontextmanager\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Callable\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import auth, db, settings\nfrom app.routes import admin as admin_routes\nfrom app.routes import auth as auth_routes\nfrom app.routes import pages as page_routes\nfrom app.routes import posts as post_routes\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\n\ndef create_app(database: Path | None = None,\n               clock: Callable[[], float] | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    settings.require_safe_secret_key()\n    database = Path(database or settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n\n    @asynccontextmanager\n    async def lifespan(_: FastAPI):\n        db.init_db(database)\n        yield\n\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\", lifespan=lifespan,\n                  dependencies=[Depends(auth.csrf_protect)])\n    app.state.database = database\n    app.state.clock = clock or time.time\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       session_cookie=\"cms_session\", same_site=\"lax\",\n                       max_age=None)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(auth.NotSignedIn)\n    async def to_login(request: Request, exc: auth.NotSignedIn):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\n    app.include_router(auth_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(admin_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(post_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(page_routes.router)\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Later ticket routers plug in here, above the catch-all.\n    app.include_router(admin_routes.catch_all)  # keep LAST\n\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n\"\"\"The admin console's home page and the /admin catch-all.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import auth, db, pages, posts, users\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\")\n\n# Include this router LAST: an unknown /admin address still sends anonymous\n# visitors to the login page instead of a bare 404.\ncatch_all = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef admin_home(request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    is_admin = user.role == \"admin\"\n    counts = posts.count_by_status(conn, None if is_admin else user.id)\n    if is_admin:\n        for status, n in pages.count_by_status(conn).items():\n            counts[status] += n\n    active, deactivated = users.count_by_active(conn) if is_admin else (None, None)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/hello.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Admin\", \"user\": user, \"drafts\": counts[posts.DRAFT],\n         \"published\": counts[posts.PUBLISHED], \"active_users\": active,\n         \"deactivated_users\": deactivated},\n    )\n\n\n@catch_all.api_route(\"/{rest:path}\", include_in_schema=False,\n                     methods=[\"GET\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"PATCH\", \"DELETE\"],\n                     dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_user)])\ndef admin_unknown(rest: str):\n    raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n\"\"\"Console routes for Pages. President-only: every route is behind the admin guard.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import auth, db, pages\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.posts import DRAFT, PUBLISHED, slugify\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/pages\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_role(\"admin\"))])\n\n\nNAV_ERROR = \"Navigation order must be a whole number, 0 or more\"\n\n\ndef _page(conn, page_id: int) -> pages.Page:\n    page = pages.get_page(conn, page_id)\n    if page is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No such Page\")\n    return page\n\n\ndef _int(value: str) -> int:\n    \"\"\"The navigation order as a number; anything that is not 0 or more counts as -1.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        return max(int(value.strip() or 0), -1)\n    except ValueError:\n        return -1\n\n\ndef _editor(request, user, page, title, body, nav_order, error=None, status_code=200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/page_edit.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit Page\" if page else \"New Page\", \"user\": user, \"page\": page,\n         \"form_title\": title, \"form_body\": body, \"form_nav_order\": nav_order,\n         \"preview_html\": render_markdown(body), \"error\": error},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef page_list():\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts?type=page\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef page_new(request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user)):\n    return _editor(request, user, None, \"\", \"\", 0)\n\n\n@router.post(\"\")\ndef page_create(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                nav_order: str = Form(\"0\"), user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        return _editor(request, user, None, title, body, nav_order, \"A Page needs a title\", 400)\n    if _int(nav_order) < 0:\n        return _editor(request, user, None, title, body, nav_order, NAV_ERROR, 400)\n    page_id = pages.create_page(conn, title, body, _int(nav_order), auth.now(request))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}\")\ndef page_edit(page_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n              conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    page = _page(conn, page_id)\n    return _editor(request, user, page, page.title, page.body, page.nav_order)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}\")\ndef page_update(page_id: int, request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                nav_order: str = Form(\"0\"), slug: str | None = Form(None),\n                user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    page = _page(conn, page_id)\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        return _editor(request, user, page, title, body, nav_order, \"A Page needs a title\", 400)\n    if _int(nav_order) < 0:\n        return _editor(request, user, page, title, body, nav_order, NAV_ERROR, 400)\n    new_slug = slugify(slug) if slug and slug.strip() else None\n    if new_slug and pages.slug_taken(conn, new_slug, page_id):\n        return _editor(request, user, page, title, body, _int(nav_order),\n                       f\"The slug “{new_slug}” is already used by another Page\", 400)\n    pages.update_page(conn, page_id, title, body, _int(nav_order), auth.now(request), new_slug)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\nCONFIRMS = {\n    \"publish\": (\"Publish this?\", \"Yes, publish it\", DRAFT, PUBLISHED,\n                \"It becomes Live at the next Publish run.\"),\n    \"unpublish\": (\"Are you sure you want to unpublish this?\", \"Yes, unpublish it\",\n                  PUBLISHED, DRAFT,\n                  \"It returns to Draft and leaves the public site at the next Publish run.\"),\n}\n\n\ndef _confirm(action: str, page_id: int, request, user, conn):\n    heading, button, _, _, note = CONFIRMS[action]\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/page_confirm.html\",\n        {\"title\": heading, \"user\": user, \"page\": _page(conn, page_id), \"action\": action,\n         \"heading\": heading, \"button\": button, \"note\": note})\n\n\ndef _change(action: str, page_id: int, request, conn):\n    _, _, from_status, to_status, _ = CONFIRMS[action]\n    if _page(conn, page_id).status == from_status:\n        pages.set_status(conn, page_id, to_status, auth.now(request))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}/publish\")\ndef page_publish_confirm(page_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                         conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _confirm(\"publish\", page_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}/publish\")\ndef page_publish(page_id: int, request: Request, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change(\"publish\", page_id, request, conn)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}/unpublish\")\ndef page_unpublish_confirm(page_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                           conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _confirm(\"unpublish\", page_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}/unpublish\")\ndef page_unpublish(page_id: int, request: Request, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change(\"unpublish\", page_id, request, conn)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}/delete\")\ndef page_delete_confirm(page_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                        conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/page_delete.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Delete Page\", \"user\": user, \"page\": _page(conn, page_id)})\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}/delete\")\ndef page_delete(page_id: int, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    pages.delete_page(conn, _page(conn, page_id).id)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts?type=page\", status_code=303)\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\nclass FakeClock:\n    \"\"\"Injected clock so idle sign-out is tested without waiting.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, now: float = 1_000_000.0):\n        self.now = now\n\n    def __call__(self) -> float:\n        return self.now\n\n    def advance(self, seconds: float) -> None:\n        self.now += seconds\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef clock() -> FakeClock:\n    return FakeClock()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef make_app(tmp_path, clock):\n    \"\"\"Factory for a fresh app on its own seeded database and the fake clock.\"\"\"\n    from app.seed import seed_users\n\n    def _make():\n        db = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n        application = create_app(database=db, clock=clock)\n        seed_users(db, {role: (u[\"email\"], u[\"password\"])\n                        for role, u in DEMO_USERS.items()})\n        return application\n\n    return _make\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(make_app) -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(make_app())\n\n\ndef csrf_of(client: TestClient, path: str = \"/login\") -> str:\n    \"\"\"Read the CSRF token out of the form on `path`.\"\"\"\n    html = client.get(path).text\n    return re.search(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"', html).group(1)\n\n\ndef sign_in(client: TestClient, email: str, password: str, token: str | None = None):\n Every admin route goes through here.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\nfrom jinja2 import pass_context\n\nfrom app /login.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef ensure_csrf(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n        request.session[\"csrf\"] = token\n    return token\n\n\n@pass_context\ndef csrf_token(context) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Use as {{ csrf_token() }} inside every state-changing form.\"\"\"\n    return ensure_csrf(context[\"request\"])\n\n\nasync def csrf_protect(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"App-wide: any request that is not GET/HEAD/OPTIONS needs a valid token.\"\"\"\n    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    if request.url.path.startswith(\"/admin\") and \"user_id\" not in request.session:\n        raise NotSignedIn  # anonymous: send to login rather than a CSRF error\n    form = await request.form()\n    sent = str(form.get(\"csrf_token\", \"\"))\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\", \"\")\n    if not expected or not secrets.compare_digest(sent, expected):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Invalid or missing CSRF token\")\n\n\ndef now(request: Request) -> float:\n    return request.app.state.clock()\n\n\ndef start_session(request: Request, user: users.User) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()  # fresh session on sign-in\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user.id\n    request.session[\"last_seen\"] = now(request)\n    ensure_csrf(request)\n\n\ndef end_session(request: Request) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()\n\n\ndef require_user(request: Request, conn=Depends(db.get_db)) -> users.User:\n    \"\"\"Signed in, not idle too long, and still active; otherwise to the login page.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    last_seen = request.session.get(\"last_seen\")\n    current = now(request)\n    if user_id is None or last_seen is None or current - last_seen > settings.IDLE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:\n        end_session(request)\n        raise NotSignedIn\n    user = users.get_user(conn, user_id)\n    if user is None or not user.active:\n        end_session(request)\n        raise NotSignedIn\n    request.session[\"last_seen\"] = current\n    return user\n\n\ndef require_role(*roles: str):\n    \"\"\"Guard for role-restricted routes: Depends(require_role(\"admin\")).\"\"\"\n\n    def guard(user: users.User = Depends(require_user)) -> users.User:\n        if user.role not in roles:\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"You do not have access to this page\")\n        return user\n\n    return guard\n\"\"\"Users: argon2-hashed passwords, roles, the active flag.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom functools import cache\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass User:\n    id: int\n    email: str\n    role: str  # \"admin\" (President) or \"editor\" (Officer)\n    active: bool\n\n\ndef normalize_email(email: str) -> str:\n    return email.strip().lower()\n\n\ndef _row_to_user(row: sqlite3.Row) -> User:\n    return User(row[\"id\"], row[\"email\"], row[\"role\"], bool(row[\"active\"]))\n\n\ndef create_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str, password: str, role: str) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO users (email, password_hash, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\",\n            (normalize_email(email), _hasher.hash(password), role),\n        )\n\n\ndef get_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> User | None:\n    row = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()\n    return _row_to_user(row) if row else None\n\n\ndef count_by_active(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> tuple[int, int]:\n    \"\"\"(active, Deactivated) User counts.\"\"\"\n    row = conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT COALESCE(SUM(active), 0) AS a, COUNT(*) AS n FROM users\").fetchone()\n    return row[\"a\"], row[\"n\"] - row[\"a\"]\n\n\ndef email_exists(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (normalize_email(email),)\n    ).fetchone() is not None\n\n\n@cache\ndef _dummy_hash() -> str:\n    return _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")\n\n\ndef authenticate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str, password: str) -> User | None:\n    \"\"\"Return the User for a correct email and password, else None.\n\n    Unknown email, wrong password and Deactivated all look the same to the\n    caller, and unknown emails still cost one hash check so timing does not\n    reveal which accounts exist.\n    \"\"\"\n    row = conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (normalize_email(email),)\n    ).fetchone()\n    stored = row[\"password_hash\"] if row else _dummy_hash()\n    try:\n        _hasher.verify(stored, password)\n    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):\n        return None\n    if row is None or not row[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return _row_to_user(row)\n\"\"\"SQLite access. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport time\nfrom contextlib import asynccontextmanager\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Callable\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import auth, db, settings\nfrom app.routes import admin as admin_routes\nfrom app.routes import auth as auth_routes\nfrom app.routes import pages as page_routes\nfrom app.routes import posts as post_routes\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\n\ndef create_app(database: Path | None = None,\n               clock: Callable[[], float] | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    settings.require_safe_secret_key()\n    database = Path(database or settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n\n    @asynccontextmanager\n    async def lifespan(_: FastAPI):\n        db.init_db(database)\n        yield\n\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\", lifespan=lifespan,\n                  dependencies=[Depends(auth.csrf_protect)])\n    app.state.database = database\n    app.state.clock = clock or time.time\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       session_cookie=\"cms_session\", same_site=\"lax\",\n                       max_age=None)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(auth.NotSignedIn)\n    async def to_login(request: Request, exc: auth.NotSignedIn):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\n    app.include_router(auth_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(admin_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(post_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(page_routes.router)\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %}</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"{{ css_path | default('style.css') }}\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header><a href=\"{{ home_path | default('index.html') }}\">{{ title }}</a></header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n  <footer><small>IPHS 400 Mini-Project #2</small></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Delete this Page?</h1>\n  <p>Are you sure you want to permanently delete “{{ page.title }}”? This cannot be undone.</p>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/pages/{{ page.id }}/delete\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Yes, delete it</button>\n    <a href=\"/admin/pages/{{ page.id }}\">Cancel</a>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\n\"\"\"T05: President-only Pages. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nfrom app import db, pages\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of\nfrom tests.test_t03_president_control import act, president_for\n\n\ndef create_page(c, title=\"About\", body=\"We are KCFC.\", order=\"2\"):\n    token = csrf_of(c, \"/admin/pages/new\")\n    return c.post(\"/admin/pages\", data={\"title\": title, \"body\": body, \"nav_order\": order,\n                                        \"csrf_token\": token}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef page_url(response) -> str:\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    return response.headers[\"location\"]\n\n\ndef test_president_creates_edits_and_sees_a_page(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    url = page_url(create_page(admin))\n    assert re.fullmatch(r\"/admin/pages/\\d+\", url)\n    html = admin.get(url).text\n    assert \"About\" in html and \"Status: Draft\" in html and \"about\" in html\n    token = csrf_of(admin, url)\n    admin.post(url, data={\"title\": \"About us\", \"body\": \"New\", \"nav_order\": \"5\",\n                          \"csrf_token\": token}, follow_redirects=False)\n    html = admin.get(url).text\n    assert \"About us\" in html and 'value=\"5\"' in html\n\n\ndef test_slug_is_generated_once_and_editable_by_president(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    url = page_url(create_page(admin, title=\"Join Us\"))\n    token = csrf_of(admin, url)\n    admin.post(url, data={\"title\": \"Join the club\", \"body\": \"\", \"nav_order\": \"1\",\n                          \"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert 'value=\"join-us\"' in admin.get(url).text\n    admin.post(url, data={\"title\": \"Join the club\", \"body\": \"\", \"nav_order\": \"1\",\n                          \"slug\": \"Get Involved\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert 'value=\"get-involved\"' in admin.get(url).text\n\n\ndef test_navigation_order_is_stored_on_the_page(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    url = page_url(create_page(admin, order=\"7\"))\n    conn = db.connect(admin.app.state.database)\n    pid = int(url.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[1])\n    assert pages.get_page(conn, pid).nav_order == 7\ndomain.md\nissue-tracker.md\ntriage-labels.md","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5a62e49e-8ab4-4254-aba3-e4ae1e5595ee","permissionDecision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reasonType":"mode"},"session_id":"93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14","version":"2.1.300","gitBranch":"main"}
