extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Hello admin</h1>\n  <h2>Dashboard</h2>\n  <p>Signed in as {{ user.email }} ({{ user.role }}).</p>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts/new\">New Post</a> · <a href=\"/admin/posts\">Content list</a>{% if user.role == 'admin' %} · <a href=\"/admin/posts?type=page\">Pages</a> · <a href=\"/admin/pages/new\">New Page</a>{% endif %}</p>\n  <ul>\n    <li>Drafts: {{ drafts }}</li>\n    <li>Published: {{ published }}</li>\n    {% if active_users is not none %}\n    <li>Active Users: {{ active_users }}</li>\n    <li>Deactivated Users: {{ deactivated_users }}</li>\n    {% endif %}\n  </ul>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/logout\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Sign out</button>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\n\"\"\"Posts: Markdown body, Draft/Published status, author, immutable slug.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\nDRAFT = \"draft\"\nPUBLISHED = \"published\"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Post:\n    id: int\n    title: str\n    slug: str\n    body: str\n    status: str\n    author_id: int\n    author_email: str\n    created_at: float\n    updated_at: float\n\n\n_SELECT = \"\"\"\nSELECT p.*, u.email AS author_email\nFROM posts p JOIN users u ON u.id = p.author_id\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _row_to_post(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Post:\n    return Post(row[\"id\"], row[\"title\"], row[\"slug\"], row[\"body\"], row[\"status\"],\n                row[\"author_id\"], row[\"author_email\"], row[\"created_at\"], row[\"updated_at\"])\n\n\ndef slugify(title: str) -> str:\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", title.lower()).strip(\"-\") or \"post\"\n\n\ndef _unique_slug(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str) -> str:\n    base = slugify(title)\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,)).fetchone():\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef create_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str, body: str, author_id: int,\n                now: float) -> int:\n    \"\"\"New Posts are always Drafts.\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO posts (title, slug, body, status, author_id, created_at, updated_at)\"\n            \" VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (title, _unique_slug(conn, title), body, DRAFT, author_id, now, now),\n        )\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef get_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> Post | None:\n    row = conn.execute(_SELECT + \" WHERE p.id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n    return _row_to_post(row) if row else None\n\n\ndef list_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection, author_id: int | None = None,\n               status: str | None = None) -> list[Post]:\n    \"\"\"Newest first. author_id limits the list to one User's Posts, status to one status.\"\"\"\n    clauses, args = [], []\n    if author_id is not None:\n        clauses.append(\"p.author_id = ?\")\n        args.append(author_id)\n    if status is not None:\n        clauses.append(\"p.status = ?\")\n        args.append(status)\n    where = \" WHERE \" + \" AND \".join(clauses) if clauses else \"\"\n    rows = conn.execute(_SELECT + where + \" ORDER BY p.created_at DESC, p.id DESC\",\n                        args).fetchall()\n    return [_row_to_post(r) for r in rows]\n\n\ndef count_by_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection, author_id: int | None = None) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Posts per status (draft, published), optionally for one author.\"\"\"\n    sql, args = \"SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS n FROM posts\", ()\n    if author_id is not None:\n        sql, args = sql + \" WHERE author_id = ?\", (author_id,)\n    found = {r[\"status\"]: r[\"n\"] for r in conn.execute(sql + \" GROUP BY status\", args)}\n    return {DRAFT: found.get(DRAFT, 0), PUBLISHED: found.get(PUBLISHED, 0)}\n\n\ndef update_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, title: str, body: str,\n                now: float, status: str, slug: str | None = None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"The slug changes only when a normalised `slug` is passed (Admin-only callers).\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, body = ?, status = ?, updated_at = ?,\"\n            \" slug = COALESCE(?, slug) WHERE id = ?\",\n            (title, body, status, now, slug, post_id),\n        )\n\n\ndef set_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, status: str, now: float) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                     (status, now, post_id))\n\n\ndef slug_taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, slug: str, post_id: int) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE slug = ? AND id != ?\",\n                        (slug, post_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef delete_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,))\n\"\"\"Create the demo President and Officer. Safe to run repeatedly.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom app import db, users\n\n\ndef seed_users(database: Path, accounts: dict[str, tuple[str, str]]) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"accounts maps role -> (email, password). Existing emails are left alone.\n\n    Returns the emails that were created. Passwords are hashed by users.create_user\n    and are never printed or stored as text.\n    \"\"\"\n    db.init_db(database)\n    conn = db.connect(database)\n    created = []\n    for role, (email, password) in accounts.items():\n        if not users.email_exists(conn, email):\n            users.create_user(conn, email, password, role)\n            created.append(email)\n    conn.close()\n    return created\n\"\"\"T03: Presidents publish, unpublish and delete Posts. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of, sign_in\nfrom tests.test_t02_posts import create_post, post_url\n\n\ndef president_for(editor) -> TestClient:\n    \"\"\"A President on the same app and database as `editor`.\"\"\"\n    admin = TestClient(editor.app)\n    sign_in(admin, \"admin@example.test\", \"test-admin-pw\")\n    return admin\n\n\ndef act(c, url, action, **extra):\n    token = csrf_of(c, url)\n    return c.post(f\"{url}/{action}\", data={\"csrf_token\": token, **extra},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef published_post(editor, title=\"Pitch: NVDA\"):\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor, title=title))\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    assert act(admin, url, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Published\" in admin.get(url).text\n    return url, admin\n\n\ndef test_president_publishes_a_draft_after_a_confirm(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor))\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    confirm = admin.get(url + \"/publish\")\n    assert confirm.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Publish this?\" in confirm.text\n    assert 'name=\"csrf_token\"' in confirm.text\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in admin.get(url).text  # a GET publishes nothing\n    assert act(admin, url, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Status: Published\" in admin.get(url).text\n\ncommit 11f808264e0649777565f9fbadc227b77a6276d5\nAuthor: Your Full Name <your-github-email@gmail.com>\nDate: {"parentUuid":"a71f0113-7fbd-42d8-90ae-aa6f074eef64","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cg3MgzpduFgQr9HkoLmhe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01M3Z1xvUdwacGZqzKLzUzdH","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat >> app/users.py <<'EOF'\n\n\nROLES = (\"admin\", \"editor\")\nEMAIL_DOMAIN = \"@kenyon.edu\"\nMIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 8\n\n\nclass GuardRailError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"A change the rules forbid; the message is shown to the President.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef list_users(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[User]:\n    rows = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users ORDER BY active DESC, email\").fetchall()\n    return [_row_to_user(r) for r in rows]\n\n\ndef _active_presidents(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> int:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM users WHERE role = 'admin' AND active = 1\").fetchone()[0]\n\n\ndef _keeps_a_president(conn: sqlite3.Connection, target: User) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"False when `target` is the last active President.\"\"\"\n    return not (target.role == \"admin\" and target.active and _active_presidents(conn) <= 1)\n\n\ndef set_role(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int, role: str) -> None:\n    target = get_user(conn, user_id)\n    if target is None or role not in ROLES:\n        raise GuardRailError(\"Unknown User or role\")\n    if role != \"admin\" and not _keeps_a_president(conn, target):\n        raise GuardRailError(\"The last active President cannot be demoted\")\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET role = ? WHERE id = ?\", (role, user_id))\n\n\ndef set_active(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int, active: bool, acting_id: int) -> None:\n    target = get_user(conn, user_id)\n    if target is None:\n        raise GuardRailError(\"Unknown User\")\n    if not active:\n        if user_id == acting_id:\n            raise GuardRailError(\"You cannot deactivate yourself\")\n        if not _keeps_a_president(conn, target):\n            raise GuardRailError(\"The last active President cannot be deactivated\")\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET active = ? WHERE id = ?\", (int(active), user_id))\nEOF\ncat > app/routes/users.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Console routes for User management. President-only: every route is behind the admin guard.\n\nThere is deliberately no delete route: Users are only ever Deactivated.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import auth, db, users\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/users\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_role(\"admin\"))])\n\n\ndef _list(request, user, conn, error=None, form_email=\"\", form_role=\"editor\", status_code=200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/user_list.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Users\", \"user\": user, \"users\": users.list_users(conn), \"error\": error,\n         \"form_email\": form_email, \"form_role\": form_role, \"roles\": users.ROLES},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef user_list(request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _list(request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.post(\"\")\ndef user_create(request: Request, email: str = Form(\"\"), role: str = Form(\"editor\"),\n                password: str = Form(\"\"), user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    email = users.normalize_email(email)\n\n    def fail(message):\n        return _list(request, user, conn, message, email, role, 400)\n\n    if not email.endswith(users.EMAIL_DOMAIN) or len(email) == len(users.EMAIL_DOMAIN):\n        return fail(f\"Use a Kenyon email address ending {users.EMAIL_DOMAIN}\")\n    if role not in users.ROLES:\n        return fail(\"Choose a role\")\n    if len(password) < users.MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH:\n        return fail(f\"The first password needs at least {users.MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH} characters\")\n    try:\n        users.create_user(conn, email, password, role)\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n        return fail(\"A User with that email already exists\")\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/users\", status_code=303)\n\n\ndef _change(request: Request, user, conn, user_id: int, apply) -> object:\n    if users.get_user(conn, user_id) is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No such User\")\n    try:\n        apply()\n    except users.GuardRailError as exc:\n        return _list(request, user, conn, str(exc), status_code=400)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/users\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{user_id}/role\")\ndef user_role(user_id: int, request: Request, role: str = Form(\"\"),\n              user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change(request, user, conn, user_id, lambda: users.set_role(conn, user_id, role))\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{user_id}/deactivate\")\ndef user_deactivate(user_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                    conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change(request, user, conn, user_id,\n                   lambda: users.set_active(conn, user_id, False, user.id))\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{user_id}/reactivate\")\ndef user_reactivate(user_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                    conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change(request, user, conn, user_id,\n                   lambda: users.set_active(conn, user_id, True, user.id))\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/user_list.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Users</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin\">Dashboard</a></p>\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <table>\n    <thead><tr><th>Email</th><th>Role</th><th>Status</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>\n    <tbody>\n    {% for u in users %}\n      <tr>\n        <td>{{ u.email }}</td>\n        <td>\n          <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/users/{{ u.id }}/role\">\n            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n            <select name=\"role\" aria-label=\"Role for {{ u.email }}\">\n              {% for r in roles %}<option value=\"{{ r }}\"{% if r == u.role %} selected{% endif %}>{{ 'Admin' if r == 'admin' else 'Editor' }}</option>{% endfor %}\n            </select>\n            <button type=\"submit\">Change role</button>\n          </form>\n        </td>\n        <td>{{ 'Active' if u.active else 'Deactivated' }}</td>\n        <td>\n          <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/users/{{ u.id }}/{{ 'deactivate' if u.active else 'reactivate' }}\">\n            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n            <button type=\"submit\">{{ 'Deactivate' if u.active else 'Reactivate' }}</button>\n          </form>\n        </td>\n      </tr>\n    {% endfor %}\n    </tbody>\n  </table>\n  <h2>New User</h2>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/users\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n    <label>Kenyon email <input type=\"email\" name=\"email\" value=\"{{ form_email }}\" required></label>\n    <label>Role\n      <select name=\"role\">\n        {% for r in roles %}<option value=\"{{ r }}\"{% if r == form_role %} selected{% endif %}>{{ 'Admin' if r == 'admin' else 'Editor' }}</option>{% endfor %}\n      </select>\n    </label>\n    <label>First password <input type=\"password\" name=\"password\" autocomplete=\"new-password\" required></label>\n    <button type=\"submit\">Create User</button>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\nEOF\npython3 - <<'EOF'\nimport re\np='app/main.py'; s=open(p).read()\ns=s.replace(\"from app.routes import posts as post_routes\\n\",\"from app.routes import posts as post_routes\\nfrom app.routes import users as user_routes\\n\")\ns=s.replace(\"    app.include_router(page_routes.router)\\n\",\"    app.include_router(page_routes.router)\\n    app.include_router(user_routes.router)\\n\")\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\np='templates/admin/hello.html'; s=open(p).read()\ns=s.replace(\"New Page</a>{% endif %}\",\"New Page</a> · <a href=\\\"/admin/users\\\">Users</a>{% endif %}\")\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ngit diff --stat","description":"Implement user management"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}]}}

{"parentUuid":"0ec4ee01-8eca-405b-ba03-3dd259f3fea1","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cg3MiS3U5s3hyuEMmfuJa","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013wd47HGY2NTzY5srCD21hd","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > tests/test_t06_users.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"T06: President-only User management. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import db, users\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of, sign_in\nfrom tests.test_t02_posts import create_post, post_url\nfrom tests.test_t03_president_control import president_for\n\n\ndef make_user(admin, email=\"new@kenyon.edu\", role=\"editor\", password=\"first-pw-123\"):\n    token = csrf_of(admin, \"/admin/users\")\n    return admin.post(\"/admin/users\", data={\"email\": email, \"role\": role,\n                                            \"password\": password, \"csrf_token\": token},\n                      follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef find(admin, email):\n    conn = db.connect(admin.app.state.database)\n    row = conn.execute(\"SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (email,)).fetchone()\n    return row[\"id\"]\n\n\ndef act(admin, email, action, **extra):\n    uid = find(admin, email)\n    token = csrf_of(admin, \"/admin/users\")\n    return admin.post(f\"/admin/users/{uid}/{action}\",\n                      data={\"csrf_token\": token, **extra}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_president_creates_a_user_with_a_hashed_first_password(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert make_user(admin).status_code == 303\n    assert \"new@kenyon.edu\" in admin.get(\"/admin/users\").text\n    conn = db.connect(admin.app.state.database)\n    stored = conn.execute(\"SELECT password_hash FROM users WHERE email = 'new@kenyon.edu'\"\n                          ).fetchone()[0]\n    assert stored.startswith(\"$argon2\") and \"first-pw-123\" not in stored\n    # signs in at once with the President-set password, no forced change\n    fresh = TestClient(admin.app)\n    assert sign_in(fresh, \"new@kenyon.edu\", \"first-pw-123\").status_code == 303\n    assert fresh.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_create_rejects_bad_input(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert make_user(admin, email=\"x@gmail.com\").status_code == 400\n    assert make_user(admin, password=\"short\").status_code == 400\n    assert make_user(admin, role=\"root\").status_code == 400\n    assert make_user(admin).status_code == 303\n    assert make_user(admin).status_code == 400  # duplicate\n\n\ndef test_president_changes_a_role(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert act(admin, \"editor@example.test\", \"role\", role=\"admin\").status_code == 303\n    conn = db.connect(admin.app.state.database)\n    assert users.get_user(conn, find(admin, \"editor@example.test\")).role == \"admin\"\n\n\ndef test_deactivated_user_cannot_sign_in_and_open_session_ends(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    assert editor.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n    assert act(admin, \"editor@example.test\", \"deactivate\").status_code == 303\n    assert editor.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303  # next action\n    fresh = TestClient(admin.app)\n    assert sign_in(fresh, \"editor@example.test\", \"test-editor-pw\").status_code != 303\n    assert fresh.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_president_reactivates(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    act(admin, \"editor@example.test\", \"deactivate\")\n    assert act(admin, \"editor@example.test\", \"reactivate\").status_code == 303\n    fresh = TestClient(admin.app)\n    assert sign_in(fresh, \"editor@example.test\", \"test-editor-pw\").status_code == 303\n    assert fresh.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_there_is_no_delete_user_action(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    uid = find(admin, \"editor@example.test\")\n    token = csrf_of(admin, \"/admin/users\")\n    for method, path in ((\"post\", f\"/admin/users/{uid}/delete\"),\n                         (\"delete\", f\"/admin/users/{uid}\")):\n        r = getattr(admin, method)(path, data={\"csrf_token\": token}, follow_redirects=False)\n        assert r.status_code in (404, 405)\n    assert \"delete\" not in admin.get(\"/admin/users\").text.lower()\n    assert find(admin, \"editor@example.test\") == uid\n\n\ndef test_deactivated_users_posts_still_show_their_name(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor, title=\"Recap: alumni call\"))\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    act(admin, \"editor@example.test\", \"deactivate\")\n    html = admin.get(url).text\n    assert \"Recap: alumni call\" in html and \"editor@example.test\" in html\n\n\ndef test_last_active_president_is_protected(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert act(admin, \"admin@example.test\", \"deactivate\").status_code == 400  # self + last\n    assert act(admin, \"admin@example.test\", \"role\", role=\"editor\").status_code == 400\n    conn = db.connect(admin.app.state.database)\n    me = users.get_user(conn, find(admin, \"admin@example.test\"))\n    assert me.active and me.role == \"admin\"\n\n\ndef test_president_cannot_deactivate_self_but_may_demote_when_another_remains(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    make_user(admin, email=\"second@kenyon.edu\", role=\"admin\")\n    assert act(admin, \"admin@example.test\", \"deactivate\").status_code == 400\n    other = TestClient(admin.app)\n    sign_in(other, \"second@kenyon.edu\", \"first-pw-123\")\n    assert act(other, \"admin@example.test\", \"deactivate\").status_code == 303\n    # second is now the last active President\n    assert act(other, \"second@kenyon.edu\", \"role\", role=\"editor\").status_code == 400\n\n\ndef test_officer_is_refused_everywhere(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert \"/admin/users\" not in editor.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert editor.get(\"/admin/users\").status_code == 403\n    token = csrf_of(editor, \"/admin\")\n    uid = find(editor, \"admin@example.test\")\n    for path, data in ((\"/admin/users\", {\"email\": \"z@kenyon.edu\", \"role\": \"admin\",\n                                         \"password\": \"long-enough-pw\"}),\n                       (f\"/admin/users/{uid}/role\", {\"role\": \"editor\"}),\n                       (f\"/admin/users/{uid}/deactivate\", {}),\n                       (f\"/admin/users/{uid}/reactivate\", {})):\n        r = editor.post(path, data={**data, \"csrf_token\": token}, follow_redirects=False)\n        assert r.status_code == 403, path\n    assert users.get_user(db.connect(editor.app.state.database), uid).active\n\n\ndef test_president_dashboard_links_to_users(client_as):\n    assert \"/admin/users\" in client_as(\"admin\").get(\"/admin\").text\nEOF\nuv run pytest -q 2>&1 | tail -20; uv run ruff check . 2>&1 | tail -5","description":"Write tests, run suite and then /bin/sh \"${HOME-}/.orca/agent-hooks/claude-hook.sh\"; else { command -p cat 2>/dev/null || cat; } >/dev/null 2>&1 || :; printf '{}\\n'; fi ;; esac; fi","durationMs":48},"type":"attachment","uuid":"619da3ac-2881-4bbf-bb58-47efca6f137d","timestamp":"2026-10-15T04:03:07.004Z","session_id":"93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14","version":"2.1.300","gitBranch":"main"}
{"type":"api-request-blob","sessionId":"93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14","hash":"f790ecf3418b3b475f3aee8b8b0ee1069f03e0f4e21cf533d120c8832def5e96","message":{"role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Implementation is in; now writing the tests."},{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013wd47HGY2NTzY5srCD21hd","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms && cat > tests/test_t06_users.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"T06: President-only User management. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import db, users\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of, sign_in\nfrom tests.test_t02_posts import create_post, post_url\nfrom tests.test_t03_president_control import president_for\n\n\ndef make_user(admin, email=\"new@kenyon.edu\", role=\"editor\", password=\"first-pw-123\"):\n    token = csrf_of(admin, \"/admin/users\")\n    return admin.post(\"/admin/users\", data={\"email\": email, \"role\": role,\n                                            \"password\": password, \"csrf_token\": token},\n                      follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef find(admin, email):\n    conn = db.connect(admin.app.state.database)\n    row = conn.execute(\"SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (email,)).fetchone()\n    return row[\"id\"]\n\n\ndef act(admin, email, action, **extra):\n    uid = find(admin, email)\n    token = csrf_of(admin, \"/admin/users\")\n    return admin.post(f\"/admin/users/{uid}/{action}\",\n                      data={\"csrf_token\": token, **extra}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_president_creates_a_user_with_a_hashed_first_password(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert make_user(admin).status_code == 303\n    assert \"new@kenyon.edu\" in admin.get(\"/admin/users\").text\n    conn = db.connect(admin.app.state.database)\n    stored = conn.execute(\"SELECT password_hash FROM users WHERE email = 'new@kenyon.edu'\"\n                          ).fetchone()[0]\n    assert stored.startswith(\"$argon2\") and \"first-pw-123\" not in stored\n    # signs in at once with the President-set password, no forced change\n    fresh = TestClient(admin.app)\n    assert sign_in(fresh, \"new@kenyon.edu\", \"first-pw-123\").status_code == 303\n    assert fresh.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_create_rejects_bad_input(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert make_user(admin, email=\"x@gmail.com\").status_code == 400\n    assert make_user(admin, password=\"short\").status_code == 400\n    assert make_user(admin, role=\"root\").status_code == 400\n    assert make_user(admin).status_code == 303\n    assert make_user(admin).status_code == 400  # duplicate\n\n\ndef test_president_changes_a_role(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert act(admin, \"editor@example.test\", \"role\", role=\"admin\").status_code == 303\n    conn = db.connect(admin.app.state.database)\n    assert users.get_user(conn, find(admin, \"editor@example.test\")).role == \"admin\"\n\n\ndef test_deactivated_user_cannot_sign_in_and_open_session_ends(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    assert editor.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n    assert act(admin, \"editor@example.test\", \"deactivate\").status_code == 303\n    assert editor.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303  # next action\n    fresh = TestClient(admin.app)\n    assert sign_in(fresh, \"editor@example.test\", \"test-editor-pw\").status_code != 303\n    assert fresh.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_president_reactivates(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    act(admin, \"editor@example.test\", \"deactivate\")\n    assert act(admin, \"editor@example.test\", \"reactivate\").status_code == 303\n    fresh = TestClient(admin.app)\n    assert sign_in(fresh, \"editor@example.test\", \"test-editor-pw\").status_code == 303\n    assert fresh.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_there_is_no_delete_user_action(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    uid = find(admin, \"editor@example.test\")\n    token = csrf_of(admin, \"/admin/users\")\n    for method, path in ((\"post\", f\"/admin/users/{uid}/delete\"),\n                         (\"delete\", f\"/admin/users/{uid}\")):\n        r = getattr(admin, method)(path, data={\"csrf_token\": token}, follow_redirects=False)\n        assert r.status_code in (404, 405)\n    assert \"delete\" not in admin.get(\"/admin/users\").text.lower()\n    assert find(admin, \"editor@example.test\") == uid\n\n\ndef test_deactivated_users_posts_still_show_their_name(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor, title=\"Recap: alumni call\"))\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    act(admin, \"editor@example.test\", \"deactivate\")\n    html = admin.get(url).text\n    assert \"Recap: alumni call\" in html and \"editor@example.test\" in html\n\n\ndef test_last_active_president_is_protected(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert act(admin, \"admin@example.test\", \"deactivate\").status_code == 400  # self + last\n    assert act(admin, \"admin@example.test\", \"role\", role=\"editor\").status_code == 400\n    conn = db.connect(admin.app.state.database)\n    me = users.get_user(conn, find(admin, \"admin@example.test\"))\n    assert me.active and me.role == \"admin\"\n\n\ndef test_president_cannot_deactivate_self_but_may_demote_when_another_remains(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    make_user(admin, email=\"second@kenyon.edu\", role=\"admin\")\n    assert act(admin, \"admin@example.test\", \"deactivate\").status_code == 400\n    other = TestClient(admin.app)\n    sign_in(other, \"second@kenyon.edu\", \"first-pw-123\")\n    assert act(other, \"admin@example.test\", \"deactivate\").status_code == 303\n    # second is now the last active President\n    assert act(other, \"second@kenyon.edu\", \"role\", role=\"editor\").status_code == 400\n\n\ndef test_officer_is_refused_everywhere(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert \"/admin/users\" not in editor.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert editor.get(\"/admin/users\").status_code == 403\n    token = csrf_of(editor, \"/admin\")\n    uid = find(editor, \"admin@example.test\")\n    for path, data in ((\"/admin/users\", {\"email\": \"z@kenyon.edu\", \"role\": \"admin\",\n                                         \"password\": \"long-enough-pw\"}),\n                       (f\"/admin/users/{uid}/role\", {\"role\": \"editor\"}),\n                       (f\"/admin/users/{uid}/deactivate\", {}),\n                       (f\"/admin/users/{uid}/reactivate\", {})):\n        r = editor.post(path, data={**data, \"csrf_token\": token}, follow_redirects=False)\n        assert r.status_code == 403, path\n    assert users.get_user(db.connect(editor.app.state.database), uid).active\n\n\ndef test_president_dashboard_links_to_users(client_as):\n    assert \"/admin/users\" in client_as(\"admin\").get(\"/admin\").text\nEOF\nuv run pytest -q 2>&1 | tail -20; uv run ruff check . 2>&1 | tail -5","description":"Write tests, run suite and lint"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}]}}

{"parentUuid":"aacb24f7-d532-4b60-a6c5-04b6295dd9c5","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cg3MmcRNvveJL6VBjqBps","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Mu2957VyfZ8zM986Wancfw","name":"Skill","input":{"skill":"code-review","args":"since HEAD (uncommitted T06 work), spec issue #1, ticket issue #7"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":564,"cache_read_input_tokens":69248,"output_tokens":110,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":564,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"global","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":110,"cache_read_input_tokens":69248,"cache_creation_input_tokens":564,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":564},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard","fallback_credit":null},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01Mu2957VyfZ8zM986Wancfw":{"skill":"code-review","args":"since HEAD (uncommitted T06 work), spec issue #1, ticket issue #7"}},"apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011Cg3Mmc62thGx6TYMegpF3","requestRef":"11286d42-3b13-4122-911a-fd1ca9dac4f8","requestedModel":"claude-sonnet-5-5","attributionSkill":"implement","type":"assistant","uuid":"780cc544-5c07-4bf0-b56c-9cb64892a50d","timestamp":"2026-10-15T04:03:27.133Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14","version":"2.1.300","gitBranch":"main"}
{"type":"api-request-blob","sessionId":"93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14","hash":"15462530d5295a89e07bb2b40a82715ee2b89bead3e4172018f9dead5710971f","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"toolu_01Mu2957VyfZ8zM986Wancfw","content":"Launching skill: code-review"},{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/.claude/skills/code-review\n\nTwo-axis review of the diff between `HEAD` and a fixed point the user supplies:\n\n- **Standards**: does the code conform to this repo's documented coding standards?\n- **Spec**: does the code faithfully implement the originating issue / spec?\n\nBoth axes run as **parallel sub-agents** so they don't pollute each other's context, then this skill aggregates their findings.\n\nThe issue tracker should have been provided to you. If not, tell the user to run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills`.\n\n## Process\n\n### 1. Pin the fixed point\n\nWhatever the user said is the fixed point (a commit SHA, branch name, tag, `main`, `HEAD~5`, etc.). If they didn't specify one, ask for it.\n\nCapture the diff command once: `git diff <fixed-point>...HEAD` (three-dot, so the comparison is against the merge-base). Also note the list of commits via `git log <fixed-point>..HEAD --oneline`.\n\nBefore going further, confirm the fixed point resolves (`git rev-parse <fixed-point>`) and the diff is non-empty. A bad ref or empty diff should fail here, not inside two parallel sub-agents.\n\n### 2. Identify the spec source\n\nLook for the originating spec, in this order:\n\n1. Issue references in the commit messages (`#123`, `Closes #45`, GitLab `!67`, etc.), fetched via the workflow in the tracker doc.\n2. A path the user passed as an argument.\n3. A spec file under `docs/`, `specs/`, or `.scratch/` matching the branch name or feature.\n4. If nothing is found, ask the user where the spec is. If they say there isn't one, the **Spec** sub-agent will skip and report \"no spec available\".\n\n### 3. Identify the standards sources\n\nSearch the repo for every file that documents how code should be written. When `CODING_STANDARDS.md` or `CONTRIBUTING.md` exists, it must be on the list.\n\nOn top of whatever the repo documents, the Standards axis always carries the **smell baseline** below: a fixed set of Fowler code smells (_Refactoring_, ch.3) that applies even when a repo documents nothing. Two rules bind it:\n\n- **The repo overrides.** A documented repo standard always wins; where it endorses something the baseline would flag, suppress the smell.\n- **Always a judgement call.** Each smell is a labelled heuristic (\"possible Feature Envy\"), never a hard violation. Like any standard here, skip anything tooling already enforces.\n\nEach smell reads *what it is* → *how to fix*; match it against the diff:\n\n- **Mysterious Name**: a function, variable, or type whose name doesn't reveal what it does or holds. → rename it; if no honest name comes, the design's murky.\n- **Duplicated Code**: the same logic shape appears in more than one hunk or file in the change. → extract the shared shape, call it from both.\n- **Feature Envy**: a method that reaches into another object's data more than its own. → move the method onto the data it envies.\n- **Data Clumps**: the same few fields or params keep travelling together (a type wanting to be born). → bundle them into one type, pass that.\n- **Primitive Obsession**: a primitive or string standing in for a domain concept that deserves its own type. → give the concept its own small type.\n- **Repeated Switches**: the same `switch`/`if`-cascade on the same type recurs across the change. → replace with polymorphism, or one map both sites share.\n- **Shotgun Surgery**: one logical change forces scattered edits across many files in the diff. → gather what changes together into one module.\n- **Divergent Change**: one file or module is edited for several unrelated reasons. → split so each module changes for one reason.\n- **Speculative Generality**: abstraction, parameters, or hooks added for needs the spec doesn't have. → delete it; inline back until a real need shows.\n- **Message Chains**: long `a.b().c().d()` navigation the caller shouldn't depend on. → hide the walk behind one method on the first object.\n- **Middle Man**: a class or function that mostly just delegates onward. → cut it, call the real target direct.\n- **Refused Bequest**: a subclass or implementer that ignores or overrides most of what it inherits. → drop the inheritance, use composition.\n\n### 4. Spawn both sub-agents in parallel\n\nIssue both sub-agent calls together, in the foreground, and aggregate the reports they return.\n\n**Standards sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The full diff command and commit list.\n- The list of standards-source files you found in step 3, **plus the smell baseline from step 3** pasted in full (the sub-agent has no other access to it).\n- The brief: \"Report, per file/hunk where relevant, (a) every place the diff violates a documented standard: cite the standard (file + the rule); and (b) any baseline smell you spot: name it and quote the hunk. Distinguish hard violations from judgement calls: documented-standard breaches can be hard, but baseline smells are always judgement calls, and a documented repo standard overrides the baseline. Skip anything tooling enforces. Under 400 words.\"\n\n**Spec sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The diff command and commit list.\n- The path or fetched contents of the spec.\n- The brief: \"Report: (a) requirements the spec asked for that are missing or partial; (b) behaviour in the diff that wasn't asked for (scope creep); (c) requirements that look implemented but where the implementation looks wrong. Quote the spec line for each finding. Under 400 words.\"\n\nIf the spec is missing, skip the Spec sub-agent and note this in the final report.\n\n### 5. Aggregate\n\nPresent the two reports under `## Standards` and `## Spec` headings, verbatim or lightly cleaned. Do **not** merge or rerank findings, because the two axes are deliberately separate (see _Why two axes_).\n\nEnd with a one-line summary: total findings per axis, and the worst issue _within each axis_ (if any). Don't pick a single winner across axes: that's the reranking the separation exists to prevent.\n\n## Why two axes\n\nA change can pass one axis and fail the other:\n\n- Code that follows every standard but implements the wrong thing → **Standards pass, Spec fail.**\n- Code that does exactly what the issue asked but breaks the project's conventions → **Spec pass, Standards fail.**\n\nReporting them separately stops one axis from masking the other.\n\n\nARGUMENTS: since HEAD (uncommitted T06 work), spec issue #1, ticket issue #7"}]}}

{"parentUuid":"2c683d4d-2559-4027-863e-b7bac99c20fb","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"268bd096-eea3-427d-901e-7ff4f0decc0a","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/.claude/skills/code-review\n\nTwo-axis review of the diff between `HEAD` and a fixed point the user supplies:\n\n- **Standards**: does the code conform to this repo's documented coding standards?\n- **Spec**: does the code faithfully implement the originating issue / spec?\n\nBoth axes run as **parallel sub-agents** so they don't pollute each other's context, then this skill aggregates their findings.\n\nThe issue tracker should have been provided to you. If not, tell the user to run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills`.\n\n## Process\n\n### 1. Pin the fixed point\n\nWhatever the user said is the fixed point (a commit SHA, branch name, tag, `main`, `HEAD~5`, etc.). If they didn't specify one, ask for it.\n\nCapture the diff command once: `git diff <fixed-point>...HEAD` (three-dot, so the comparison is against the merge-base). Also note the list of commits via `git log <fixed-point>..HEAD --oneline`.\n\nBefore going further, confirm the fixed point resolves (`git rev-parse <fixed-point>`) and the diff is non-empty. A bad ref or empty diff should fail here, not inside two parallel sub-agents.\n\n### 2. Identify the spec source\n\nLook for the originating spec, in this order:\n\n1. Issue references in the commit messages (`#123`, `Closes #45`, GitLab `!67`, etc.), fetched via the workflow in the tracker doc.\n2. A path the user passed as an argument.\n3. A spec file under `docs/`, `specs/`, or `.scratch/` matching the branch name or feature.\n4. If nothing is found, ask the user where the spec is. If they say there isn't one, the **Spec** sub-agent will skip and report \"no spec available\".\n\n### 3. Identify the standards sources\n\nSearch the repo for every file that documents how code should be written. When `CODING_STANDARDS.md` or `CONTRIBUTING.md` exists, it must be on the list.\n\nOn top of whatever the repo documents, the Standards axis always carries the **smell baseline** below: a fixed set of Fowler code smells (_Refactoring_, ch.3) that applies even when a repo documents nothing. Two rules bind it:\n\n- **The repo overrides.** A documented repo standard always wins; where it endorses something the baseline would flag, suppress the smell.\n- **Always a judgement call.** Each smell is a labelled heuristic (\"possible Feature Envy\"), never a hard violation. Like any standard here, skip anything tooling already enforces.\n\nEach smell reads *what it is* → *how to fix*; match it against the diff:\n\n- **Mysterious Name**: a function, variable, or type whose name doesn't reveal what it does or holds. → rename it; if no honest name comes, the design's murky.\n- **Duplicated Code**: the same logic shape appears in more than one hunk or file in the change. → extract the shared shape, call it from both.\n- **Feature Envy**: a method that reaches into another object's data more than its own. → move the method onto the data it envies.\n- **Data Clumps**: the same few fields or params keep travelling together (a type wanting to be born). → bundle them into one type, pass that.\n- **Primitive Obsession**: a primitive or string standing in for a domain concept that deserves its own type. → give the concept its own small type.\n- **Repeated Switches**: the same `switch`/`if`-cascade on the same type recurs across the change. → replace with polymorphism, or one map both sites share.\n- **Shotgun Surgery**: one logical change forces scattered edits across many files in the diff. → gather what changes together into one module.\n- **Divergent Change**: one file or module is edited for several unrelated reasons. → split so each module changes for one reason.\n- **Speculative Generality**: abstraction, parameters, or hooks added for needs the spec doesn't have. → delete it; inline back until a real need shows.\n- **Message Chains**: long `a.b().c().d()` navigation the caller shouldn't depend on. → hide the walk behind one method on the first object.\n- **Middle Man**: a class or function that mostly just delegates onward. → cut it, call the real target direct.\n- **Refused Bequest**: a subclass or implementer that ignores or overrides most of what it inherits. → drop the inheritance, use composition.\n\n### 4. Spawn both sub-agents in parallel\n\nIssue both sub-agent calls together, in the foreground, and aggregate the reports they return.\n\n**Standards sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The full diff command and commit list.\n- The list of standards-source files you found in step 3, **plus the smell baseline from step 3** pasted in full (the sub-agent has no other access to it).\n- The brief: \"Report, per file/hunk where relevant, (a) every place the diff violates a documented standard: cite the standard (file + the rule); and (b) any baseline smell you spot: name it and quote the hunk. Distinguish hard violations from judgement calls: documented-standard breaches can be hard, but baseline smells are always judgement calls, and a documented repo standard overrides the baseline. Skip anything tooling enforces. Under 400 words.\"\n\n**Spec sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The diff command and commit list.\n- The path or fetched contents of the spec.\n- The brief: \"Report: (a) requirements the spec asked for that are missing or partial; (b) behaviour in the diff that wasn't asked for (scope creep); (c) requirements that look implemented but where the implementation looks wrong. Quote the spec line for each finding. Under 400 words.\"\n\nIf the spec is missing, skip the Spec sub-agent and note this in the final report.\n\n### 5. Aggregate\n\nPresent the two reports under `## Standards` and `## Spec` headings, verbatim or lightly cleaned. Do **not** merge or rerank findings, because the two axes are deliberately separate (see _Why two axes_).\n\nEnd with a one-line summary: total findings per axis, and the worst issue _within each axis_ (if any). Don't pick a single winner across axes: that's the reranking the separation exists to prevent.\n\n## Why two axes\n\nA change can pass one axis and fail the other:\n\n- Code that follows every standard but implements the wrong thing → **Standards pass, Spec fail.**\n- Code that does exactly what the issue asked but breaks the project's conventions → **Spec pass, Standards fail.**\n\nReporting them separately stops one axis from masking the other.\n\n\nARGUMENTS: since HEAD (uncommitted T06 work), spec issue #1, ticket issue #7"}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"8493e64c-e6e7-4aa5-a6cd-d020fb6157bd","timestamp":"2026-10-15T04:03:27.215Z","sourceToolUseID":"toolu_01Mu2957VyfZ8zM986Wancfw","session_id":"93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14","version":"2.1.300","gitBranch":"main"}

The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>**Review of the uncommitted T06 diff: no hard violations found. The rest are judgement calls.**\n\nI only read the diff and the standards files. I did not run the tests.\n\n### (a) Standards\n\nChecked against `CLAUDE.md` Hard constraints, `SECURITY-CHECKLIST.md` and `CONTEXT.md`:\n\n- **CSRF:** all four POST forms in `templates/admin/user_list.html` carry `csrf_token()`. The tests post with a token. I did not confirm the server-side CSRF check, which is presumably middleware.\n- **Role enforcement:** `app/routes/users.py` guards the whole router with `require_role(\"admin\")`, so it is enforced in code, not only by the link in `hello.html` (checklist rules 4 and 10). `test_officer_is_refused_everywhere` covers all four POST routes and the GET.\n- **Users never deleted:** the diff has no delete route. `test_there_is_no_delete_user_action` checks that.\n- **Passwords:** the form field is never echoed back. The test asserts the stored hash starts with `$argon2`.\n- **Relative paths:** this rule covers published HTML only. The absolute `/admin/...` paths here are in the admin console, like the existing `hello.html`.\n- **Anonymous access:** `tests/test_t06_users.py` has no test that an anonymous request to `/admin/users` redirects to login (checklist rule 5). The router guard probably covers it.\n\n### (b) Smells (all judgement calls)\n\n- **Mysterious Name** in `app/routes/users.py`:\n  - `def _list(request, user, conn, error=None, ...)`: it renders the page, so `_render_user_list` fits better.\n  - `def fail(message)` and `apply` (a callback parameter) are also vague.\n  - In the tests, `act` and `find` could be `post_user_action` and `user_id_for`.\n- **Data Clumps** in `app/routes/users.py`: `(request, user, conn)` is passed through `_list` and `_change`, and `_list` takes 7 parameters (`error, form_email, form_role, status_code` on top).\n- **Duplicated Code**:\n  - `get_user` is called three times per change: in `_change`, then in `users.set_role` or `set_active`. The 404 check in the route duplicates the \"Unknown User\" guard in the domain module.\n  - `templates/admin/user_list.html` has the same `{{ 'Admin' if r == 'admin' else 'Editor' }}` option loop twice.\n- **Repeated Switches / Primitive Obsession:** role strings `'admin'` and `'editor'` are literals in `app/users.py` (`_active_presidents` SQL, `set_role`) and in the template's `'Admin' if ...`. The role-label mapping belongs next to `ROLES`.\n- **Divergent Change:** the email-domain, role and password-length validation sits in the `user_create` route. The guard rails sit in `app/users.py`. `set_role` re-validates the role, so the role check exists in both places. Moving create validation into `users.create_user` would keep one home for the rules.\n- **Placement:** `ROLES`, `EMAIL_DOMAIN` and `MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH` are defined mid-module in `app/users.py`, after existing functions.\n- **Weak check, a correctness nit:** `email.endswith(\"@kenyon.edu\")` accepts `a@b@kenyon.edu`. The `type=\"email\"` input only covers the browser side.\n- **Race condition, minor:** `set_role` and `set_active` check \"last President\" and then update in separate steps. That is fine for a single-user laptop console.\n- **Vocabulary:**\n  - Error strings say \"President\" while the dropdown says \"Admin\". That matches `CONTEXT.md`, where every President holds the Admin role.\n  - The \"Choose a role\" and \"Unknown User or role\" messages are vague.\n- **Tests:**\n  - `tests/test_t06_users.py` imports helpers from `tests.test_t02_posts` and `tests.test_t03_president_control`, which couples test modules.\n  - `assert \"delete\" not in admin.get(\"/admin/users\").text.lower()` is fragile. It will fail on any future text containing \"delete\".\n  - `admin.app.state.database` is a Message Chain, repeated in `find`, `act` and several tests.\n- **`app/routes/users.py`:** the return annotation `-&gt; object` on `_change` is inconsistent. The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>**Review of the uncommitted T06 diff (issue #7, spec #1 capability 6, CONTEXT.md)**\n\nI read the code only. I did not run the tests.\n\n**(a) Missing or partial**\n- Ticket: \"a President cannot deactivate themselves\". Spec, Users decision: \"cannot deactivate **or demote** yourself\". `set_role` has no self check. `test_president_cannot_deactivate_self_but_may_demote_when_another_remains` asserts a self-demote succeeds. The ticket and capability 6 name only self-deactivation, and the Implementation Decisions line is stricter. The spec disagrees with itself, and the code took the looser reading. A President who demotes themselves is cut off from `/admin/users` at once. Settle this in the spec or CONTEXT.md.\n- Ticket: \"A Deactivated User's Posts still show their name as author\". `posts.py` only joins `u.email AS author_email`, and the `users` table has no name column. The test can only check an email. This predates T06, but nothing in the diff gives a Post a name. Capability 7 says the public byline never shows an email.\n- Ticket: \"Commit starts 'T06:' ... `/code-review` findings are posted on this issue first\". Nothing is committed yet, and I can't tell whether the findings are posted.\n\n**(b) Scope creep**\n- `MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 8` is not in the ticket or spec. The spec says only that the President sets the first password.\n- The `@kenyon.edu` suffix check is reasonable for \"Kenyon email\". It still accepts malformed addresses such as `a@b@kenyon.edu`, and it would block a legitimate non-`@kenyon.edu` alumni or test address.\n- The users list sorts active first, then by email. This is harmless.\n\n**(c) Implemented but looks wrong or fragile**\n- Guard rails are otherwise sound. `_keeps_a_president` only blocks when the target is an active admin and the active-admin count is 1. Demoting or deactivating a Deactivated admin is allowed, which is fine. Reactivation is unguarded, which is correct.\n- Last-President demotion: `set_role` blocks `role != \"admin\"` only when the target is the last active President. Re-submitting the same role passes. Good.\n- Session ending: `require_user` re-reads the User on every request. If the User is missing or inactive it clears the session and raises `NotSignedIn`. That meets \"refused at their next action\". `authenticate` also rejects inactive Users, with no timing leak.\n- Officer refusal: the router-level `Depends(require_role(\"admin\"))` covers GET and every POST, including `/{id}/role`, `/deactivate`, `/reactivate` and unknown ids. The guard runs before the 404 lookup, so an Officer always gets 403. An anonymous POST goes to login through the CSRF hook. Direct URL access is covered.\n- No delete: there is no delete route or button, and the router docstring says so. `test_there_is_no_delete_user_action` should probe `POST /admin/users/{id}/delete` and `DELETE /admin/users/{id}` and expect 404/405.\n- Nothing stops a guard-rail failure from re-rendering the list with status 400 and a redirect-less POST. That is fine, but a page refresh then resubmits the form.\n- The last-active check is a read-then-write with no transaction. This is a low risk on a single-user local console.</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>50640</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>4</tool_uses><duration_ms>25086</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"}

I did not run the tests.\n\n**(a) Missing or partial**\n- Ticket: \"a President cannot deactivate themselves\". Spec, Users decision: \"cannot deactivate **or demote** yourself\". `set_role` has no self check. `test_president_cannot_deactivate_self_but_may_demote_when_another_remains` asserts a self-demote succeeds. The ticket and capability 6 name only self-deactivation, and the Implementation Decisions line is stricter. The spec disagrees with itself, and the code took the looser reading. A President who demotes themselves is cut off from `/admin/users` at once. Settle this in the spec or CONTEXT.md.\n- Ticket: \"A Deactivated User's Posts still show their name as author\". `posts.py` only joins `u.email AS author_email`, and the `users` table has no name column. The test can only check an email. This predates T06, but nothing in the diff gives a Post a name. Capability 7 says the public byline never shows an email.\n- Ticket: \"Commit starts 'T06:' ... `/code-review` findings are posted on this issue first\". Nothing is committed yet, and I can't tell whether the findings are posted.\n\n**(b) Scope creep**\n- `MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 8` is not in the ticket or spec. The spec says only that the President sets the first password.\n- The `@kenyon.edu` suffix check is reasonable for \"Kenyon email\". It still accepts malformed addresses such as `a@b@kenyon.edu`, and it would block a legitimate non-`@kenyon.edu` alumni or test address.\n- The users list sorts active first, then by email. This is harmless.\n\n**(c) Implemented but looks wrong or fragile**\n- Guard rails are otherwise sound. `_keeps_a_president` only blocks when the target is an active admin and the active-admin count is 1. Demoting or deactivating a Deactivated admin is allowed, which is fine. Reactivation is unguarded, which is correct.\n- Last-President demotion: `set_role` blocks `role != \"admin\"` only when the target is the last active President. Re-submitting the same role passes. Good.\n- Session ending: `require_user` re-reads the User on every request. If the User is missing or inactive it clears the session and raises `NotSignedIn`. That meets \"refused at their next action\". `authenticate` also rejects inactive Users, with no timing leak.\n- Officer refusal: the router-level `Depends(require_role(\"admin\"))` covers GET and every POST, including `/{id}/role`, `/deactivate`, `/reactivate` and unknown ids. The guard runs before the 404 lookup, so an Officer always gets 403. An anonymous POST goes to login through the CSRF hook. Direct URL access is covered.\n- No delete: there is no delete route or button, and the router docstring says so. `test_there_is_no_delete_user_action` should probe `POST /admin/users/{id}/delete` and `DELETE /admin/users/{id}` and expect 404/405.\n- Nothing stops a guard-rail failure from re-rendering the list with status 400 and a redirect-less POST. That is fine, but a page refresh then resubmits the form.\n- The last-active check is a read-then-write with no transaction. {"parentUuid":"33f3f4af-83c4-45c2-9e8d-26bada52c416","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"2d9f55b1-30c1-404a-bb5f-e4937fd75d46","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"<task-notification>\n<task-id>acb77e384f04e06bf</task-id>\n<tool-use-id>toolu_01QAFKHtahT4NWyj1J5ULJ18</tool-use-id>\n<output-file>/private/tmp/claude-503/-Users-biswas1-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14/tasks/acb77e384f04e06bf.output</output-file>\n<status>completed</status>\n<summary>Agent \"Spec review of T06\" finished</summary>\n<note>A task-notification fires each time this agent stops with no live background children of its own. The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>**Review of the uncommitted T06 diff (issue #7, spec #1 capability 6, CONTEXT.md)**\n\nI read the code only. I did not run the tests.\n\n**(a) Missing or partial**\n- Ticket: \"a President cannot deactivate themselves\". Spec, Users decision: \"cannot deactivate **or demote** yourself\". `set_role` has no self check. `test_president_cannot_deactivate_self_but_may_demote_when_another_remains` asserts a self-demote succeeds. The ticket and capability 6 name only self-deactivation, and the Implementation Decisions line is stricter. The spec disagrees with itself, and the code took the looser reading. A President who demotes themselves is cut off from `/admin/users` at once. Settle this in the spec or CONTEXT.md.\n- Ticket: \"A Deactivated User's Posts still show their name as author\". `posts.py` only joins `u.email AS author_email`, and the `users` table has no name column. The test can only check an email. This predates T06, but nothing in the diff gives a Post a name. Capability 7 says the public byline never shows an email.\n- Ticket: \"Commit starts 'T06:' ... `/code-review` findings are posted on this issue first\". Nothing is committed yet, and I can't tell whether the findings are posted.\n\n**(b) Scope creep**\n- `MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 8` is not in the ticket or spec. The spec says only that the President sets the first password.\n- The `@kenyon.edu` suffix check is reasonable for \"Kenyon email\". It still accepts malformed addresses such as `a@b@kenyon.edu`, and it would block a legitimate non-`@kenyon.edu` alumni or test address.\n- The users list sorts active first, then by email. This is harmless.\n\n**(c) Implemented but looks wrong or fragile**\n- Guard rails are otherwise sound. `_keeps_a_president` only blocks when the target is an active admin and the active-admin count is 1. Demoting or deactivating a Deactivated admin is allowed, which is fine. Reactivation is unguarded, which is correct.\n- Last-President demotion: `set_role` blocks `role != \"admin\"` only when the target is the last active President. Re-submitting the same role passes. Good.\n- Session ending: `require_user` re-reads the User on every request. If the User is missing or inactive it clears the session and raises `NotSignedIn`. That meets \"refused at their next action\". `authenticate` also rejects inactive Users, with no timing leak.\n- Officer refusal: the router-level `Depends(require_role(\"admin\"))` covers GET and every POST, including `/{id}/role`, `/deactivate`, `/reactivate` and unknown ids. The guard runs before the 404 lookup, so an Officer always gets 403. An anonymous POST goes to login through the CSRF hook. Direct URL access is covered.\n- No delete: there is no delete route or button, and the router docstring says so. `test_there_is_no_delete_user_action` should probe `POST /admin/users/{id}/delete` and `DELETE /admin/users/{id}` and expect 404/405.\n- Nothing stops a guard-rail failure from re-rendering the list with status 400 and a redirect-less POST. That is fine, but a page refresh then resubmits the form.\n- The last-active check is a read-then-write with no transaction. This is a low risk on a single-user local console.</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>50640</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>4</tool_uses><duration_ms>25086</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"},"uuid":"28bc205c-c257-46e9-acee-9520ccb948a4","timestamp":"2026-10-15T04:04:04.351Z","permissionMode":"bypassPermissions","origin":{"kind":"task-notification","producer":"session-task","runId":"0mv90kivx-b1dc4b8c"},"promptSource":"system","turnOrigin":"task_notification","turnPosition":{"promptIndex":1,"turnIndex":3},"queueSkipAttachments":true,"userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14","version":"2.1.300","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"a7644e9c-4cc6-44d0-8aab-15577a4dd4e8","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cg3MrFVgfvFU8PTpcUX5X","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01UYgX4WWk5pJ2KsKVvRquyS","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > /private/tmp/claude-503/-Users-biswas1-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14/scratchpad/review.md <<'EOF'\n## /code-review findings for T06 and how each was resolved\n\n### Spec\n- **Self-demotion** (spec #1: \"cannot deactivate or demote yourself\"): the first cut allowed it. **Fixed**: `set_role` now refuses a President changing their own role; test `test_president_cannot_deactivate_or_demote_self`.\n- **Posts show the author's name after Deactivation**: Posts join to `users` with no active filter, so the author still resolves. There is no name column yet, so the author is shown by email in the console. Covered by `test_deactivated_users_posts_still_show_their_name`. **Not changed**: a display name and the public byline belong to the public-site ticket.\n- **Scope**: the 8-character minimum first password and the `@kenyon.edu` check are small additions beyond the ticket text. **Kept**: they follow \"Kenyon email\" and basic password hygiene.\n- **Email check accepted `a@b@kenyon.edu`**: **Fixed**, plus a test.\n- Guard rails, session end at the next action, Officer refusal on every route and no delete action were checked and are correct.\n\n### Standards\nNo hard violations. CSRF on all forms, argon2 hashes, the admin role guard on the whole router, no delete route.\n- Judgement-call smells (`_list` takes 7 parameters, the role-label loop is duplicated in the template, `ROLES` etc. sit mid-module, test helpers are imported across test modules): **not changed**, because the code is small and matches the surrounding style.\n- Fragile `\"delete\" not in text` test assertion: **Fixed**; it now checks for `/delete`.\n- Read-then-write race on the last-President check: **accepted**, since the console is a single-laptop app.\nEOF\ngh issue comment 7 --body-file /private/tmp/claude-503/-Users-biswas1-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14/scratchpad/review.md && git add -A && git commit -q -F - <<'EOF' && git push 2>&1 | tail -2\nT06: User management and deactivation\n\nPresident-only Users list, create (Kenyon email, role, first password),\nchange role, Deactivate and reactivate. No delete action. {"parentUuid":"71604de6-103f-48e0-b466-9eef8781c974","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cg3Ms6cAWzFh7pwUdvNyD","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"T06 (issue #7) is committed and pushed to `origin/main` as `5e28616`, and the code-review findings are posted as a comment on the issue. The full suite passes with 96 tests. I didn't run `ruff` because it isn't installed in this environment.\n\n**Checklist**\n- DONE: President-only Users list, create (Kenyon email, role, first password stored as an argon2 hash), change role, deactivate and reactivate. There is no delete action.\n- DONE: guard rails. The last active President can't be deactivated or demoted, and a President can't deactivate or demote themselves.\n- DONE: a Deactivated User can't sign in, and a signed-in one is refused at their next action.\n- DONE: Officers get no Users link and are refused with a 403 on `/admin/users` and every user action. Tests cover all of this through `client_as`.\n- DONE: `gh auth status` passed before the push, and the findings comment went up before the commit.\n- SKIPPED: `.claude/state/phase` and `PROJECT_STATE.md`. I set the phase file to `T06`, but it isn't tracked, so it isn't in the commit. The repo has no `PROJECT_STATE.md`.\n\n**Review fixes**\n- The first version let a President demote themselves, but the spec says they can't. I fixed that.\n- I tightened the email check so `a@b@kenyon.edu` is rejected.\n- I made the \"no delete\" test check for `/delete` in the page instead of the word \"delete\".\n\n**Worth knowing**\n- Posts have no display-name field, so a Deactivated User's Posts still show the author by email in the console. The public \"By <name>\" byline needs a name field, which I left for the public-site ticket.\n- I added an 8-character minimum for the first password. The ticket doesn't ask for it.\n- The commit also picked up `notes/usage-ledger.csv`, which was already modified, per the repo rule that those files go in every commit."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1185,"cache_read_input_tokens":80571,"output_tokens":636,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1185,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"global","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":636,"cache_read_input_tokens":80571,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1185,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1185},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard","fallback_credit":null},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011Cg3Ms6MXmxKdYMwcVXn7K","requestRef":"7d761864-7327-4210-b623-be38cb6a13d9","requestedModel":"claude-sonnet-5-5","type":"assistant","uuid":"b43f0cd2-ab1c-4b4c-b8b6-f8399dde68ce","timestamp":"2026-10-15T04:04:44.887Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"93e84ca8-3329-4e56-9c9e-33437f5d2b14","version":"2.1.300","gitBranch":"main"}
